------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE altcart
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE altcart
Local lnRec, lnSum
Select TempChq
lnRec = Iif(Not Eof([TempChq]), Recno([TempChq]), 0)
Count to lnSum For Not IsEmpty(TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.NContas + TempChq.NCheques)
If (lnRec > 0)
	Goto (lnRec)
EndIf
Return Empty(lnSum)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE ativabotoes
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE ativabotoes
LParameters pInsere

Local llVisible

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cmdGrpBotoes
	.Visible = !pInsere
	.Enabled = !pInsere
	
	If .Visible
		.CmdFPagto.Enabled     = (crSigCdOpt.Parcelas='S' And InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'CONSULTAR','INSERIR','ALTERAR'))
		.CmdProtocolos.Enabled = (crSigCdOpt.EmiProts='S' And Thisform.pcEscolha='CONSULTAR')
		.CmdImprime.Enabled    = InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'CONSULTAR')
		.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE calculasaldo
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE calculasaldo
Local Ret
Ret = fRecalculaS( .t.,ThisForm.Podatamgr )
Return Ret
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE contacorrente
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE contacorrente
LParameters llvoper

Local ldDtContab, _pData

&& SigMvCcr.ContaPgs = 2 para que na Consulta Genérica de Vendas, o lançamento em SigMvCcr continue
&& sendo apontado como Pendente, mas no Pagamento / Recebimento o Cheque não possa ser trocado.

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

If crSigCdOpt.DtContab=2 && Contabilizar na Data de Vencimento
	ldDtContab = xPar.Vencs
Else && Contabilizar na Data de Lançamento
	ldDtContab = xPar.Datas
Endif
* Obs.: Todos os "xPar.Datas" Abaixo (Na Rotina) Foram Trocados Por "ldDtContab"
********************************************************************************

=Seek( xPar.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' )
_doc = 'cheques'

If !Empty( crSigOpFp.Grupods + crSigOpFp.Contads )
	lnValor    = Abs( xpar.Valos )
	_GrupoDest = crSigOpFp.Grupods
	_ContaDest = crSigOpFp.Contads
	_GrupoOrig = crSigChLcq.Grupos
	_ContaOrig = crSigChLcq.Contas
	_MoeOrig   = crSigChLcq.Moeds
	_CotOrig   = crSigChLcq.Cotas
	_CotDest   = Iif(!Empty(xPar.CotFPgs),xPar.CotFpgs,1)
	lnoValor   = ( lnValor * _CotDest / _CotOrig )

	If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
		_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
				iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
		        ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
	Else
		_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
		If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
			_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
		Endif
	Endif
	
	_Hist = _Hist + ' (' + Alltrim( crSigOpFp.FPags ) + ')'

	_noper     = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
	lnRetVOper = _NOper
	If llVOper
		_voper = crSigChLcq.Emps + Alltrim( Str( lnRetVOper, 9 ) )
	Else
		_voper = crSigChLcq.Emps + Alltrim( str( _noper, 9 ) )
	Endif

	If crSigOpFp.Trocos = 1
		xsoper = iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR','C','D')
		xoper  = iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR','D','C')
	Else
		xoper  = iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR','C','D')
		xsoper = iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR','D','C')
	Endif

	pCidC = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
	Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
			opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
			sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, ContaPgs, CidChaves ) ;
		Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, crSigChLcq.emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
				_hist, xoper, xsoper, _MoeOrig, _CotOrig, lnoValor, .t., ;
				_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
				crSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, xpar.CotFpgs, lnValor, xPar.VPags, 2, pcidc)

	=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
			
	ThisForm.FazContra

	Select crSigMvPar
	Replace NOpers With _NOper, DtAlts With _pData
	
	If crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1
		_NOper = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
		_GrupoDest = crSigChLcq.Grclis
		_ContaDest = crSigChLcq.Iclis
		_GrupoOrig = crSigCdPam.GrupoRecs
		_ContaOrig = crSigCdPam.ContaRecs
		
		pCidC = Dtos(ldDtContab)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ldDtContab)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
		Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Docus, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
				opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
				sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, ContaPgs, CidChaves ) ;
			Values ( _NOper, _VOper, _Doc, crSigChLcq.emps, ldDtContab, usuar, _pData, xPar.Vencs, ;
					_hist, xoper, xsoper, crSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, .t., ;
					_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
					crSigOpFp.MoeFpgs, 1, lnValor, xPar.VPags, 2, pcidc)

		=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				
		ThisForm.FazContra
	EndIf
Endif

If llVOper
	Return lnRetVOper
Else
	Return
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE criacursor
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE criacursor
Local lnCampos, lnI

If Used('xPar')
	Use In xPar
EndIf

Select *, Space(34) as Leituras From CrSigMvPar Where 0=1 ;
Into Cursor xPar ReadWrite
Select xPar
Index On Vencs Tag Vencs
Index On Bancos+Agencias+Contas+Numeros Tag Chq

Select *,Space(3) as MoeComs From CrSigMvCcr Where 0=1 ;
Into Cursor TmpMccr ReadWrite
Select TmpMccr
Index on Grupos+Contas+Hists+Moedas+MoeComs+Hist2s Tag Comissao

Create Cursor CliNeg (Iclis c(10), Situas c(3))
Index On Iclis Tag Iclis

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE deletar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE deletar
Private ldDat

Local lnLotePar, lcNopers, Codigo
Local lcMoedas, ldDatas, lcSGrupos, lcSContas, lcSMoedas
Local lcOpEntradas, _pData,lcOldEmps,lcOldNumos,lcOldCodigos,lcOldGrupos, lcOldContas

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

Declare LaCampo(4,2)
Declare LaCampo1(2,2)
Declare laCampo3(2,2)

*** Excluíndo Informações
Create Cursor DelLote ( Nums C(8) )
Codigo    = crSigChLcq.numlotes
lnLotePar = crSigChLcq.NumLoteFPs
=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
lcOpEntradas = iif(Not Empty(crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas),crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas,Padr('ENTRADA',Len(crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas)))

If crSigCdOpt.Parcelas='S'
	If !Empty(lnLotePar)
		lcDelChm = [Delete From SigCqChm Where NumLotes = ]+Str(lnLotePar)
		lnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelChm,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
	lcWher  = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Emps = ']+CrSigChLcq.Emps+[' And NumloteChs = ]+Str(Codigo)
	lnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select CrPar
	Go Top
	Do While !Eof()
		lcCheque = CrPar.Bancos + CrPar.Agencias + CrPar.Contas + CrPar.Numeros
		If !Empty(crSigChLcq.Emps) And !Empty(CrPar.Nopers)
			lcVOpers = crSigChLcq.Emps + AllTrim(Str(CrPar.Nopers))
			lcWher   = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Vopers = ']+lcVOpers+[']
			lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
			Select CrMccr
			Scan
				=fRecalculaS( CrMccr.Grupos, CrMccr.Contas, CrMccr.Datas, CrMccr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				=fRecalculaS( CrMccr.sGrupos, CrMccr.sContas, CrMccr.Datas, CrMccr.sMoedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
			EndScan

			lcDelMccr = [Delete From SigMvCcr Where Vopers = ']+lcVOpers+[']
			lnErro    = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelMccr,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		EndIf
		LaCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
		LaCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
		LaCampo(3,1)='nContas'
		LaCampo(4,1)='Ncheques'
		LaCampo(1,2)=CrPar.Bancos
		LaCampo(2,2)=CrPar.Agencias
		LaCampo(3,2)=CrPar.Contas
		LaCampo(4,2)=CrPar.Numeros
		If !ThisForm.podatamgr.Chkregister('SigCqChm',@LaCampo)
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@LaCampo) And Crcheque.Umovs=lcOpEntradas
				lcDelChe = [Delete From SigChe Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
				lnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelChe,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf
			EndIf
			=fCriarCurSit(CrCheque.Iclis)
		Endif
		Select CrPar
		Skip
	EndDo

	lcDelPar = [Delete From SigMvPar Where Emps = ']+CrSigChLcq.Emps+[' And NumloteChs = ]+Str(Codigo)
	lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcDelPar,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	lcEmpChq  = CrSigChLcq.Emps
	lcLoteChq = crSigChLcq.NumLotes
	
	lcDelCar = [Delete From SigCdCar Where Emps=']+lcEmpChq+[' And NumLoteChs=]+Str(lcLoteChq)
	lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelCar,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	lcDelCart= [Delete From SigCdCtt Where Emps=']+lcEmpChq+[' And NumLoteChs=]+Str(lcLoteChq)
	lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelCart,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	lcDelCard= [Delete From SigCdCrd Where Emps=']+lcEmpChq+[' And NumLoteChs=]+Str(lcLoteChq)
	lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelCard,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
EndIf

=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')

lcWher = [Select * From SigCqChm Where NumLotes = ]+Str(codigo)
lnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcwher,'CrChm')
IF lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select CrChm
Scan
	lcVOpers = crChm.Emps + AllTrim(Str(CrChm.Numos,9))
	lcWher   = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Vopers = ']+lcVOpers+[']
	lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	Select CrMccr
	Scan
		=fRecalculaS( CrMccr.Grupos, CrMccr.Contas, CrMccr.Datas, CrMccr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
		=fRecalculaS( CrMccr.sGrupos, CrMccr.sContas, CrMccr.Datas, CrMccr.sMoedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
	EndScan

	lcDelMccr = [Delete From SigMvCcr Where Vopers = ']+lcVOpers+[']
	lnErro    = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelMccr,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	LaCampo1(1,1)='Emps'
	LaCampo1(2,1)='Nopers'
	LaCampo1(1,2)=CrChm.Emps
	LaCampo1(2,2)=CrChm.Numos
	If !Empty(CrChm.Numos) And ThisForm.Podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigCdClh',@LaCampo1)
		lcDelClih = [Delete From SigCdClh Where Emps=']+CrChm.Emps+[' And Nopers=]+Str(CrChm.Numos)+[ And Iclis = ']+CrChm.Iclis+[']
		lnErro    = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelClih,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	lcWher   = [Select * From SigChe Where Nums = ']+CrChm.Nums+[']
	lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcwher,'CrCheque')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	Select CrCheque
	Go Top
	=fCriarCurSit(CrCheque.Iclis)
	=Seek(CrCheque.umovs,'crSigCdOpt','operacao')
	If crSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1
		lcWher  = [Select Count(1) As Total From SigCqChm Where Nums=']+CrCheque.Nums+[' Group By Nums ]
		lnErro  = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CsApaga')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		Select CsApaga
		Go top
		If csApaga.Total <= 1
			lcDelChe = [Delete From SigChe Where Nums=']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDelche,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		EndIf
	EndIf
	=Seek(crSigChLcq.codigos, "crSigCdOpt", "Operacao" )
	Insert Into DelLote ( Nums ) Values ( crChm.Nums )

	Select CrChm

EndScan

lcDelChm = [Delete From SigCqChm Where NumLotes = ]+Str(codigo)
lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcDelChm,'')
IF lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf

lcDelCheh = [Delete From SigCheh Where NumLotes = ]+Str(codigo)
lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcDelCheh,'')
IF lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
EndIf

** Atualizando MOVS no SigChe
Select DelLote
Scan
	_chave = Nums
	lcWher = [Select * From SigCqChm Where Nums = ']+_Chave+[' Order By DtSis ]
	lnErro = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrChm')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf
	
	Select CrChm
	Go Top
	If Reccount() > 0
		_umov        = " "
		_cont        = 0
		lnAlineas    = 0
		lcProtocolos = ''
		lcGrupos     = ''
		lcContas     = ''
		lcOldNumos   = ''
		lcOldCodigos = ''
		lcOldGrupos  = ''
		lcOldContas  = ''
		lcOldEmps    = ''
		Scan
			_cont = _cont + 1
			_umov = codigos
			lnAlineas = IIF(NAlineas > 0, nAlineas, lnAlineas )
			lcProtocolos = Protocolos
			lcGrupos     = crChm.Grupos
			lcContas     = crChm.Contas
			lcOldEmps    = CrChm.Emps 
			lcOldNumos   = CrChm.Numos
			lcOldCodigos = CrChm.Codigos
		EndScan
		lDtAlt = _pData
		
		lcUpDate = [Update SigChe Set Umovs = ']+_Umov+[', Nalineas=]+Str(lnAlineas)+;
				   [ , Protocolos=']+lcProtocolos+[' , Grupos=']+lcGrupos+[' , Contas=']+lcContas+[', ]+;
				   [DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+_Chave+[']
		lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		lcWher = [Select * From SigChe Where Nums = ']+_Chave+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcwher,'CrCheque')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		Select CrCheque
		Go Top

		If Not Empty( crCheque.FPags ) And Seek( crCheque.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' ) And ( crSigCdOpt.BxPends = 'S' )
			_Cheque = CrCheque.bancos+CrCheque.agencias+CrCheque.ncontas+Crcheque.ncheques
			laCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
			laCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
			laCampo(3,1)='ncontas'
			laCampo(4,1)='ncheques'
			laCampo(1,2)=Crcheque.Bancos
			laCampo(2,2)=Crcheque.Agencias
			laCampo(3,2)=Crcheque.ncontas
			laCampo(4,2)=Crcheque.ncheques
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPbx','CrSigCdPbx',@laCampo)
				Select CrSigCdPbx
				Scan For Valos # 0
					lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Emps=']+CrSigCdPbx.Emps+[' And Dopes=']+CrSigCdPbx.Dopes+[']+;
							 [ And Numes=]+Str(CrSigCdPbx.Numes,6)
				
					LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf
				
					Select CrPar
					Scan
						If _Cheque = bancos + agencias + contas + numeros
							lcpago = '1'
							ldDat = _pData
						
							lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
										  [Set VPags = 0, ] + ;
										  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
										  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
									    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']
							
							lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
							IF lnErro < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf
						Endif
					EndScan
				EndScan
			Else
				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+CrCheque.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+CrCheque.Agencias+[']+;
						 [ And contas=']+CrCheque.NContas+[' And numeros=']+CrCheque.Ncheques+[']

				LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				Select CrPar
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0
					lcpago = '1'
					ldDat = _pData
					
					lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
								  [Set VPags = 0, ] + ;
								  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
								  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
							    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']
							    
					lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf
				Endif
			EndIf
			
			If ( crSigOpFp.BxComChqs = 1 ) 
				Create Cursor TmpComiss ( Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Valos n(12,2), Moedas c(3), Nopers n(6), Cotacaos n(12,4), Contas c(10))
				Index on Emps+dopes+Str(Numes,6)+moedas+str(nopers,9) Tag Empdopnum

				_Cheque = crCheque.Bancos + crCheque.Agencias + crCheque.NContas + crCheque.NCheques
				
				lcWher  = [Select * From SigCdPbx Where Bancos=']+CrCheque.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+CrCheque.Agencias+[' ]+;
						  [And NContas=']+CrCheque.Ncontas+[' And Ncheques=']+CrCheque.NCheques+[']
				LnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPrBx')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
				laCampo3(1,2)=CrPrbx.Nopers
				laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
				laCampo3(2,2)=CrPrBx.Empos

				Select CrPrBx
				Go top
				If Reccount() > 0
					Scan For Valos # 0
						If CrPrbx.Tipos = "P" And ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar',@lacampo3)
							Select CrPar
							Go Top
							If !Seek( CrPar.Emps + CrPar.Dopes + Str( CrPar.Numes, 6 ) + CrPrbx.Moedas + Str( CrPrbx.Nopers, 9 ), 'TmpComiss' )
								Insert Into TmpComiss ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moedas, Nopers, Cotacaos, Contas  ) ;
										Values ( CrPar.Emps, CrPar.Dopes, CrPar.Numes, CrPrbx.Moedas, CrPrbx.Nopers, CrPrbx.Cotacaos, CrPrbx.Contas )
							Endif
							Replace TmpComiss.Valos With TmpComiss.Valos + CrPrbx.Valos
						Endif
						If CrPrbx.Tipocs = "P"
							If !Seek( CrPrbx.Emps + CrPrbx.Dopecs + Str( CrPrbx.Numecs, 6 ) + CrPrbx.Moecs + Str( CrPrbx.Nopercs, 9 ), 'TmpComiss' )
								Insert Into TmpComiss ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moedas, Nopers, Cotacaos, Contas ) ;
										Values ( CrPrbx.Emps, CrPrbx.Dopecs, CrPrbx.Numecs, CrPrbx.Moecs, CrPrbx.Nopercs, CrPrbx.Cotacaocs, CrPrbx.Contas )
							Endif
							Replace TmpComiss.Valos With TmpComiss.Valos + CrPrbx.Valocs
						Endif
					EndScan

					Select TmpComiss
					Scan for TmpComiss.Valos # 0 And !Empty( TmpComiss.Moedas )
						_ValBaixa = TmpComiss.Valos
						_ChaveEest = TmpComiss.Emps + TmpComiss.Dopes + Str( TmpComiss.Numes, 6 )

						laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
						laCampo3(1,2)=TmpComiss.Nopers
						laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
						laCampo3(2,2)=TmpComiss.Emps

						=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',_ChaveEest)
						=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',TmpComiss.dopes)
						=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar',@laCampo3)
						
						Select CrEest
						Go Top
						Select CrOpe
						Go top
						Select CrPar
						Go Top

						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
						If crOpe.CMoes # Tmpcomiss.Moedas
							_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa * crPar.CotFpgs
							If Empty( crOpe.CMoes )
								_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
							Else
								_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
								_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
							Endif
						Endif
						_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera

						lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCmm Where Emps=']+TmpComiss.emps+[' And Dopes=']+TmpComiss.Dopes+[' ]+;
								 [And Numes=]+Str(TmpComiss.Numes,6)
						
						LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrComis')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
						
						Select CrComis
						Scan
							_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
							Replace CrComis.Baixas With CrComis.Baixas + ;
												 Iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR',-1,1) * _Baixar
							lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrComis.Baixas,12,2)+;
									   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrComis.CidChaves+[']
							LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
							IF lnErro < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf
						Endscan
					Endscan
				Else

					_Cheque = crCheque.Bancos + crCheque.Agencias + crCheque.NContas + crCheque.NCheques

					lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+CrCheque.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+CrCheque.Agencias+[']+;
							 [ And contas=']+CrCheque.NContas+[' And Numeros=']+CrCheque.Ncheques+[']

					LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf

					Select CrPar
					Go Top
					If Reccount() > 0
						lcPago = '1'
						ldDat = _pData
						
						lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
									  [Set VPags = 0, ] + ;
									  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
									  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
								    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']
								    
						lnErro   = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
						=Seek( CrPar.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' )
						_ValBaixa = crPar.Valos
						_ChaveEest = crPar.Emps + crPar.Dopes + Str( crPar.Numes, 6 )

						=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',_ChaveEest)
						=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',CrPar.dopes)
						
						Select CrEest
						Go Top
						Select CrOpe
						Go top
						
						Select CrPar

						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
						If crOpe.CMoes # Moefpgs
							_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa * crPar.Cotfpgs
							If Empty( crOpe.CMoes )
								_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
							Else
								_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
								_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
							Endif
						Endif
						_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera

						lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCmm Where Emps=']+CrPar.emps+[' And Dopes=']+crPar.Dopes+[' ]+;
								 [And Numes=]+Str(crpar.Numes,6)
						
						LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrComis')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf

						Select CrComis
						Scan
							_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
							Replace crComis.Baixas With crComis.Baixas + ;
														 IIf(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR',-1,1) * _Baixar
							lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrComis.Baixas,12,2)+;
									   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrComis.CidChaves+[']
							LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
							IF lnErro < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf
						Endscan
					Endif
				EndIf
			EndIf

			If !Empty(lcOldNumos)
				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Emps=']+lcOldEmps+[' And Nopers=]+Str(lcOldNumos)
				ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')

				Select crMccr
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0 And !Empty(CrMccr.Vopers)
					=Seek(lcOldCodigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
					lcOldVOper = crMccr.VOpers
					lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where VOpers=']+lcOldVoper+[']
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrvMccr')
					Select CrvMccr
					Scan
						If ( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = crSigChe.GruOrigs + crSigChe.ConOrigs And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] ) Or ;
							( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = lcOldGrupos + lcOldContas And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] )
							lcpago = '1'
							lcDtps = CrvMccr.Dopcs + CrvMccr.Opers + CrvMccr.Tipos + lcPago
							ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = 0, pagos = ?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrvMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
						Endif
					Endscan
				EndIf
			Endif

		Endif
		
		If _Cont = 1
			llDep = .f.
		
			lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdPbx Set Deposits = ?llDep  Where Bancos=']+CrCheque.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+CrCheque.Agencias+[' ]+;
					   [And NContas=']+CrCheque.Ncontas+[' And Ncheques=']+CrCheque.NCheques+[']
			lnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		Endif
	Endif
Endscan

Select crSigChLcq
Set Order to NumLotes
Seek Codigo
=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')

Use in DelLote

If ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'EXCLUIR' 
	If !Empty(crSigChLcq.NumLoteFps)
		lnNumLote = CrSigChLcq.NumLoteFps

		lcDelLchm = [Delete From SigChLcq Where NumLotes = ]+Str(lnNumLote)
		lnErro    = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDellchm,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf

	lnNumLote = Codigo

	lcDelLchm = [Delete From SigChLcq Where NumLotes = ]+Str(lnNumLote)
	lnErro    = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcDellchm,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

EndIf
RETURN .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE fazcontra
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE fazcontra
Select crSigMvCcr

noper   = crSigMvCcr.nopers
voper   = crSigMvCcr.vopers
oper    = crSigMvCcr.sopers
grupo   = crSigMvCcr.sgrupos
conta   = crSigMvCcr.scontas
moed    = crSigMvCcr.smoedas
cot     = crSigMvCcr.scotacaos
valor   = crSigMvCcr.svalors
soper   = crSigMvCcr.opers
sgrupo  = crSigMvCcr.Grupos
sconta  = crSigMvCcr.Contas
smoed   = crSigMvCcr.moedas
scot    = crSigMvCcr.cotacaos
svalor  = crSigMvCcr.valors
nlanc   = crSigMvCcr.nlancs
vlanc   = crSigMvCcr.vlancs
data    = crSigMvCcr.datas
dataa   = crSigMvCcr.datalts
venc    = crSigMvCcr.vencs
valpag  = crSigMvCcr.valpags
emp     = crSigMvCcr.emps
hist    = crSigMvCcr.hists
dope    = crSigMvCcr.dopes
nume    = crSigMvCcr.numes
contapg = crSigMvCcr.ContaPgs
pCidC   = Dtos(Data)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(Data)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 

Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( VOpers, NOpers, NLancs, VLancs, Opers, Grupos,;
		Contas, Moedas, Cotacaos, Valors, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts,;
		sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, sopers, autos,;
		hists, dopes, numes, valpags, vencs, ContaPgs, CidChaves ) ;
	Values ( vOper, nOper, nLanc, vLanc, Oper, Grupo, Conta, Moed, Cot,;
		Valor, Emp, Data, Usuar, Dataa, sGrupo, sConta, sMoed, sCot,;
		sValor, sOper, .t., hist, Dope, Nume, valpag, venc, ContaPg, pCidC )

=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE fvercheque
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE fvercheque
LParameters pBanco, pAgencia, pConta, pNumero, pLimpa, pMarca

Local lcTpLeChq, lcOrder, lnRecNo, llRetorno, lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero
Local lcCheque, lcAntTipos, lcMsg
Declare LaCampo(4,2)

ThisForm.Achoulcheq = .f.

Goto Top In CrSigCdPam

lcTpLeChq = CrCfCom.TpLeChqs
=Seek(CrSigChLcq.Codigos,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')

Select TempChq
lcOrder = Order()
lnRecNo = Recno()

llRetorno = .T.
lcBanco   = Iif(Type('pBanco')   = 'C',pBanco,   Space(3)  )
lcAgencia = Iif(Type('pAgencia') = 'C',pAgencia, Space(4)  )
lcConta   = Iif(Type('pConta')   = 'C',pConta,   Space(10) )
lcNumero  = Iif(Type('pNumero')  = 'C',pNumero,  Space(6)  )
lcCheque  = Padl(Alltrim(lcBanco),3,'0') + Padl(Alltrim(lcAgencia),4,'0') + Padl(Alltrim(lcConta),10,'0') + Padl(Alltrim(lcNumero),6,'0')
lcMsg     = ''

If Not fVerDigitos(lcCheque,23)
	lcMsg = 'Leitura de cheque incorreta !!!'
Endif

If Empty(lcMsg) And Not Empty(lcCheque)
	Select TempChq
	Set Order to Cheque
	Seek lcCheque
	If !Eof() And lnRecNo = RecNo()
         Skip
	Endif
	
	If !Eof() And TempChq.Bancos = lcBanco And TempChq.Agencias = lcAgencia And ;
				  TempChq.NContas = lcConta And TempChq.NCheques = lcNumero 
		lcMsg = 'Cheque já lançado nessa movimentação !!!'
	Else
		lcSql = [Select * From SigChe Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' And ]+;
				[nContas = ']+lcConta+[' And nCheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
		OkChq = ( ThisForm.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrCheque') > 0 And Reccount('CrCheque') > 0 )
		
      	If CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1
			If OkChq And ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR'
	  			lcMsg = 'Cheque já cadastrado !!!'
			Endif
		Else
			If Empty(crSigChLcq.GruCarts) And Not Empty(CrCheque.GruCarts) And ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Get_QtdeChqs.Value = 0
				Replace GruCarts With Crcheque.GruCarts,;
						ConCarts With CrCheque.ConCarts In CrSigChLcq
			Endif

			Do Case
				Case !OkChq
					lcMsg = 'Cheque não cadastrado !!!'

				Case CrCheque.GruCarts # crSigChLcq.GruCarts Or CrCheque.ConCarts # crSigChLcq.ConCarts
					lcMsg = 'Carteira de Cheque Inválida !!! ('+CrCheque.GruCarts+'/'+CrCheque.ConCarts+')'

				Case ! Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Opants) And crCheque.Umovs # CrSigCdOpt.opants
					lcMsg = 'Operação Incorreta para este Cheque !!!'

				OtherWise
					lcWhere = [Select * From SigCqChm Where Nums=']+CrCheQue.Nums+[' Order By Nums Desc, DtSis Desc ]
					lnErro = Thisform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrChm')
					Select CrChm
					Go Top
					If ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'ALTERAR' And crChm.NumLotes = crSigChLcq.NumLotes
					Else
						If ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR'
							If Seek(crCheque.Umovs,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
								TipoChe = CrSigCdOpt.Tipos
								=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
								TipoChm = CrSigCdOpt.Tipos
								If TipoChe = TipoChm
									lcMsg = 'Tipo de Movimentação inválida para esse cheque !!!'
								EndIf
							Else
								lcMsg = 'Código de Movimentação de Cheque Não Cadastrada !' + ;
			            			   Chr(13) + 'Contate o Suporte Técnico'
							Endif
						Endif
					EndIf
			EndCase
		EndIf

		If OkChq And Empty(lcMsg)
			lcCheque = Crcheque.Bancos + '-'+CrCheque.Agencias+'-'+CrCheque.nContas+'-'+CrCheque.nCheques
			lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set nCheques = nCheques Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			If ThisForm.podatamgr.sqlexecute(lcupdate,'') < 1
				lcMsg = 'Cheque Sendo Utilizado por Outro Usuário: '+lcCheque
			EndIf
		Endif
		
		=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
		
		If Empty(lcMsg) And CrSigCdOpt.Parcelas = 'S'
			If Empty(crSigChLcq.GrClis) Or Empty(crSigChLcq.IClis)
				Select crSigChLcq
				Replace crSigChLcq.Grclis With crCheque.Grclis, ;
						crSigChLcq.IClis  With crCheque.Iclis
			Else
				If Not (crCheque.Grclis + crCheque.Iclis == crSigChLcq.Grclis + crSigChLcq.Iclis)
					lcMsg = 'Emissor do cheque inválido para essa movimentação de cheque !!!'
				Endif
			EndIf
		EndIf
	Endif

	=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
	If Not Empty(lcMsg)
		Wait Window('Passe o cheque pelo leitor,ou' + chr(13)+ ;
               '<enter> para digitar manualmente !') nowait
	EndIf
	
	If Not ThisForm.Editou

		lcquery = [Select bancos,agencias,contas,ncheques,emps,cpfs,id,dopes,ncheqfin,vencs,valors,datas,cidchaves From SigCqLcq Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' ]+;
					[And Contas = ']+lcconta+[' And Ncheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
		
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigCqLcq')
		Select CrSigCqLcq
		If Reccount() = 0 And lcBanco = '237' &&Bradesco
			lcCt2 = '000'+Right(lcConta,7)
			lcquery = [Select bancos,agencias,contas,ncheques,emps,cpfs,id,dopes,ncheqfin,vencs,valors,datas,cidchaves From SigCqLcq Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' ]+;
						[And Contas = ']+lcCt2+[' And Ncheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigCqLcq')
		Endif
		If Reccount() > 0
			Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( CrSigCqLcq.Cpfs, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
			If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
				Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 999.999.999-99" )
			Else
				Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 99.999.999/9999-99" )
			EndIf
			=ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpcli','Cpfs',Cgc1,[cpfs,Iclis,Rclis,Grupos])
			Select TempChq
			Set Order To (lcOrder)
			If lnRecNo # 0
				Goto (lnRecNo)
			Endif
			Replace Vencs  With CrSigCqLcq.Vencs,;
					Valors With CrSigCqLcq.Valors,;
					GrClis With crTmpCli.Grupos

			If Empty( TempChq.Datas) 
				Select TempChq
				If ( CrSigCdOpt.nDtEmiss = 2 )
					Replace TempChq.Datas With Tempchq.Vencs
				Endif
				Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Data_Emiss.Refresh
			Endif

			If (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1)
				Select crSigChLcq
				Replace Grclis With TempChq.Grclis, ;
						Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis
				
				With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
					.Value = Evaluate('TempChq.Cpfs')
					.Refresh
				EndWith
			EndIf

			Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crTmpCli.Iclis
			Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crTmpCli.Rclis
			Thisform.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = CrTmpCli.Cpfs

			ThisForm.Achoulcheq = .t.

		Else

			Select TempChq
			Set Order To (lcOrder)
			If lnRecNo # 0
				Goto (lnRecNo)
			Endif
				
			If Not Eof() And Not Bof()
				Skip -1
			EndIf
			
			If Not Bof() And TempChq.Bancos = lcBanco And TempChq.Agencias = lcAgencia And TempChq.nContas = lcConta

				=ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpcli','Cpfs',TempChq.cpfs,[cpfs,Iclis,Rclis,Grupos])
		
				Select TempChq
				pVenc = Gomonth(Vencs,1)
				pVlr  = Valors
				pGrp  = GrClis
				pDt   = Datas
				pIcli = Iclis
				pCpf  = Cpfs
				Skip
				If Empty(TempChq.Vencs)
					Replace Vencs  With pVenc,;
							Valors With pVlr,;
							GrClis With pGrp
					If Empty( TempChq.Datas) 
						Replace TempChq.Datas With pDt
					EndIf
				Endif

				With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
					.Value = pCpf
					.Refresh
				EndWith

				Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crTmpCli.Iclis
				Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crTmpCli.Rclis
				Thisform.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = CrTmpCli.Cpfs

			Else
			
				If ThisForm.tipoleitora = 'P' And CrSigCdOpt.Parcelas <> [S]
					Select TempChq
					Set Order To (lcOrder)
					Go Bott
					Replace Iclis with '',;
							Cpfs  With ''
					
					Replace CrSigChLcq.Iclis With '' In CrSigChLcq
					
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = Space(10)
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = ''
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Refresh
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.refresh
					Thisform.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.refresh

				Endif
			
			Endif

		Endif
	Endif
	If Empty(lcMsg) And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1
		lcCliente = CrCheque.Iclis
		lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
		=Thisform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
		Select CrTmpCli
		Go Top
		Select TempChq
		Set Order To (lcOrder)
		If lnRecNo # 0
			Goto (lnRecNo)
		Endif
		Replace TempChq.Grclis   With CrCheque.Grclis, ;
				TempChq.Iclis    With CrCheque.Iclis,  ;
				TempChq.Cpfs     With CrtmpCli.Cpfs, ;
				TempChq.Nomes    With crtmpCli.Rclis, ;
				TempChq.Valors   With crCheque.Valors, ;
				TempChq.Emps     With crCheque.Emps,   ;
				TempChq.Vencs    With Iif(Thisform.Editou,TempChq.Vencs,crCheque.Vencs),  ;
				TempChq.Datas	 With crCheque.Datas,;
				TempChq.Nums     With CrCheque.Nums

		If (CrSigCdOpt.Parcelas = 'S') And (ThisForm.Op_Escolha = 'INSERIR') And ;
				Empty(crSigChLcq.GruOrigs + crSigChLcq.ConOrigs)
			Select crSigChLcq
			Replace GruOrigs With crCheque.Grclis, ;
					ConOrigs With crCheque.Iclis							
		EndIf
		
		If CrSigCdOpt.Obs = [C] And Thisform.Podatamgr.Cursorquery('SigCheh','CrSigCheh','Nums',CrCheque.Nums)
			Select CrSigCheH
			Index On Datas Tag Datas Descending
			Go Top
			Replace TempChq.Obs With crSigCheh.Hists In TempChq
		Endif

	EndIf
EndIf

llRetorno = .T.

Select TempChq
Set Order To (lcOrder)
If lnRecNo # 0
	Goto (lnRecNo)
Endif

If !Empty(lcMsg)
	If Not pLimpa
		Thisform.Limpa_Che()	&& Limpa os campos antes de mostrar o MessageBox
	EndIf
	
	If pMarca
		Messagebox(lcMsg,0+48,'',3000)
	Else
		Messagebox(lcMsg,0+48,'')
	EndIf 
		
	llRetorno = .F.
EndIf

Select TempChq
Return llRetorno
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE imprelat
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE imprelat
Ok = Messagebox('Imprimir o codigo CMC7 ',4+48+256 ,'')

Ok2 = Messagebox('Imprimir Descrição do Destino/Emissor',4+48+256 ,'')

Create Cursor TmpRelat (Ps C(1), Bancos C(3), Agencias C(4), NContas C(10), NCheques C(6), ;
						Grclis C(10), Iclis C(10), Rclis c(40), Valors N(12,2), Protocolos C(8), ;
						Vencs D(8) null, FPags C(12), Numeros C(6), Emps C(3), Cmc7 c(36), ;
						Imprime l, Imprime2 l, marca n(1,0) )
Index On Ps+Dtos(Vencs) Tag Vencs

=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos, 'crSigCdOpt', 'Operacao')

m.Imprime  = Iif(Ok = 6,.t.,.f.)
m.Imprime2 = Iif(Ok2= 6,.t.,.f.)
m.Ps = '1'
Select TempChq
Scan
	If !Empty(TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.Ncontas + TempChq.NCheques)
		Scatter Memvar
		m.Rclis = m.Nomes
		Insert Into TmpRelat From MemVar
	EndIf
EndScan

m.Ps = '2'
If crSigCdOpt.Parcelas = 'S'
	Select xPar
	Scan
		If !Empty(xPar.FPags)
			Scatter Memvar
			m.Valors   = m.Valos
			m.NContas  = m.Contas
			m.NCheques = m.Numeros
			lcCliente = m.Iclis
			lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
			=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
			Select CrTmpCli
			Go Top
			m.Rclis = CrtmpCli.Rclis
			Insert Into TmpRelat From MemVar
		EndIf
	EndScan
EndIf

lcCliente = crSigChLcq.contas
lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos,RAZAOS From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
Select CrTmpCli
Go Top

Select TmpRelat
Go Top
Cabs = 'Movimentação de Cheques'

Report Form SigMvChq Preview NoConsole

Select TempChq
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inchequeserial
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inchequeserial
If Not Thisform.LeCheque
	Thisform.ChqLido = " "
EndIf

If InList(ThisForm.TipoLeitora,'P') And !ThisForm.IniciaLeitura
	Return
EndIf

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Leitura()
Thisform.ChqLido = Thisform.ReadComm.Campo

If Atc('?',ThisForm.ReadComm.Campo) <> 0 Or Empty(ThisForm.Chqlido)
	If !ThisForm.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen And !ThisForm.ReadComm.PortaError
		ThisForm.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .T.
	EndIf
	ThisForm.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.OutPut = 'WEBCD'
	Wait Window 'Erro na Leitura do Cheque <Tecle Enter>...'
	Wait Clear
	ThisForm.ChqLido = ''
	With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque 
		.Get_Bco.Value   = ' '
		.Get_Age.Value   = ' '
		.Get_Conta.Value = ' '
		.Get_Num.Value   = ' '
		.Get_Bco.SetFocus
	EndWith
	ThisForm.Leitor = .F.
	Return
EndIf
KeyBoard '{ENTER}'
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.Value = Thisform.ChqLido
IF ThisForm.Tipoleitora = [P]
Else
	ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.Valid
	If Empty(TempChq.Valors)
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Valor.SetFocus
	Else
		If CrSigCdOpt.Alineas = 'S'
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Alinea.SetFocus
		EndIf
	EndIf
Endif
Thisform.LeCheque = .F.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE inserir
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE inserir
Local _TotalMov, _GrupoAnt, _ContaAnt, _CotaAnt, _MoedaAnt, _ValorAnt, lcHistClis, lcGruCliH
Local lcProtocolos, lcCpfs, lnNumLote, ldDtEnt, ldDtSis, lnCotas
Local lcGruOrigs, lcConOrigs, lcGruDests, lcConDests
Local _Grupo, _Conta, COper, lcProcura, lcOldNumos, lcOldEmps, lcOldCodigos, lcOldGrupos, lcOldContas
Local lnValPends2, _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _MoedaOrig, _CotaOrig, _ValorOrig
Local _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, _MoedaDest, _CotaDest, _ValorDest
Local lcUltGruDests, lcUltConDests, lcUltMoeda, lnUltValor, lnUltCota, lcOpEntradas
Local llInsCliNoChq, llInseCheq, _pData

Declare laCampo(4,2), laCampo3(2,2), laCampo4(3,2)

=Seek(ThisForm.Locregister,'crSigChLcq','NumLotes')

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

lnNum     = '99999999'
lcCliente = Space(10)
lnLote    = CrSigChLcq.NumLotes
LnOper    = 99999999
CidChav   = Space(20)
lDtAlt    = _pData

Select CrSigChe
Zap
Select CrSigCheh
Zap
Select CrSigMvCcr
Zap
Select CrSigCqChm
Zap
Select TmpMccr
Set Order To Comissao
Zap
Select CrSigCdClh
Zap
Select CrSigMvPar
Zap
Select CrSigCdPbx
Zap
Select CrSigClLan
Zap
Select CrSigCdCar
Zap
Select CrSigCdCtt
Zap
Select CrSigCdCrd
Zap

Create Cursor TmpConsolida ( TGrupos C(10), TContas C(10), TMoedas C(3), TValors N(12,2), ;
							 TCotas N(12,4), OGrupos c(10), OContas c(10) )
Index On TGrupos+TContas+TMoedas+OGrupos+oContas Tag TGruConMoe

=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
lcOpEntradas = iif(Not Empty(crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas),crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas,Padr('ENTRADA',Len(crSigCdOpt.OpEntradas)))
lnNumLote = crSigChLcq.NumLotes
ldDtEnt   = crSigChLcq.DtEnts
ldDtSis   = crSigChLcq.DtSis

_TotalMov = 0

Select TempChq

Scan
	If Empty(Bancos+Agencias+NContas+NCheques)
		Loop
	EndIf
	
	llInsCliNoChq = .f.
	If crSigCdOpt.CadEmis = [S]
		lcCpfs = ''
		If Empty(TempChq.Iclis)
			If Not ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrCli','CPfs',TempChq.Cpfs,[Iclis,Grupos])
				lcGrPadClis = Iif(Not Empty(crSigChLcq.grclis),CrSigChLcq.GrClis,crSigCdPam.GrPadClis)

				lcCpfs = StrTran( TempChq.Cpfs, '.', '' )
				lcCpfs = StrTran( lcCpfs, '-', '' )
				lcCpfs = StrTran( lcCpfs, '/', '' )
				lcCpfs = AllTrim( lcCpfs )

				If Len(Alltrim(lcCpfs))<>14
					lnTpClis = 1
				Else
					lnTpClis = 2
				EndIf

				Select crSigCdCli
				Locate For Cpfs = TempChq.Cpfs
				If Eof()
					Append Blank
					Replace Rclis  With TempChq.Nomes ;
							Cpfs   With TempChq.Cpfs  ;
							Tpclis With lnTpClis ;
							Grupos With lcGrPadClis ;
							Dtalts With ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao)
					=Seek(lcGrPadClis,'crSigCdGcr','Codigos')
					Loja = Iif(Empty(TempChq.Emps),_Empr,TempChq.Emps)
					If crSigCdGcr.TpEmps = 1  && Inclui Empresa
						CodCli = fGerUniqueKey( Alltrim( crSigCdGcr.Codigos ) + Loja )
						Replace crSigCdCli.Iclis with Alltrim( crSigCdGcr.DgCods ) + Loja + Padl(CodCli,6,'0')
					Else && Não Inclui Empresa
						CodCli = fGerUniqueKey( Alltrim( crSigCdGcr.Codigos ) )
						Replace crSigCdCli.Iclis with Alltrim( crSigCdGcr.DgCods ) + Padl(CodCli,9,'0')
					Endif
					Replace crSigCdCli.Nclis  With CodCli, ;
							crSigCdCli.TpCads With crSigCdGcr.TpCads
				EndIf
				Select TempChq
				Replace Iclis  With crSigCdCli.Iclis , ;
						Grclis With crSigCdCli.Grupos
			Else

				Select TempChq
				Replace Iclis  With crCli.Iclis , ;
						Grclis With crCli.Grupos

			Endif
					
			llInsCliNoChq = .t.
		Endif
	EndIf

	lcOldNumos   = ''
	lcOldCodigos = ''
	lcOldGrupos  = ''
	lcOldContas  = ''
	lcOldEmps    = ''
	
	laCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
	laCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
	laCampo(3,1)='ncontas'
	laCampo(4,1)='ncheques'
	laCampo(1,2)=Tempchq.Bancos
	laCampo(2,2)=Tempchq.Agencias
	laCampo(3,2)=Tempchq.ncontas
	laCampo(4,2)=Tempchq.ncheques
	
	llInseCheq = .f.
	
	If !ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@laCampo)
		llInseCheq = .t.
		If !Empty(crSigCdOpt.FPags)
			Select crSigOpFp
			Set Order to Fpags
			Seek (crSigCdOpt.FPags)
		EndIf
		lcGrclis   = iif(crSigCdOpt.EmiInds ='S',TempChq.Grclis,crSigChLcq.Grclis)
		lcIclis    = iif(crSigCdOpt.EmiInds ='S',TempChq.Iclis,crSigChLcq.Iclis)
		lcGruOrigs = iif(crSigCdOpt.Origems = 1,crSigChLcq.GruOrigs,iif(crSigCdOpt.EmiInds='S',TempChq.Grclis,crSigChLcq.Grclis))
		lcConOrigs = iif(crSigCdOpt.Origems = 1,crSigChLcq.ConOrigs,iif(crSigCdOpt.EmiInds='S',TempChq.Iclis,crSigChLcq.Iclis))

		Select crSigChe
		_num=trans(fGerUniqueKey('busnum'),"@L 99999999")
		Append Blank
		Replace Bancos 	  With tempchq.bancos,;
				agencias  with tempchq.agencias,;
				ncontas	  with tempchq.ncontas,;
				ncheques  with tempchq.ncheques,;
				valors	  with tempchq.valors,;
				vencs  	  with tempchq.vencs,;
				Leituras  With TempChq.Leituras, ;
				nums	  with _num,;
				autos     with .f.,;
				FPags	  with crSigCdOpt.Fpags, ;
				iclis	  with lcIclis, ;
				grclis    with lcGrclis,;
				datatrans with crSigChLcq.datatrans,;
				Emps 	  with TempChq.Emps, ;
				GruCarts  with crSigChLcq.GruCarts,;
				ConCarts  with crSigChLcq.ConCarts,;
				Grures    with tempchq.grures,  ;
				Conres    with tempchq.conres,  ;
				GruOrigs  With lcGruOrigs, ;
				ConOrigs  With lcConOrigs, ;
				Grupos    With iif(Empty(crSigCdOpt.FPags),crSigCdPam.GruCheques, ;
							       iif(crSigOpFp.ValPends # 1,lcGruOrigs, crSigCdPam.GruCheques)), ;
				Contas    With iif(Empty(crSigCdOpt.FPags),crSigCdPam.ConCheques, ;
								   iif(crSigOpFp.ValPends # 1,lcConOrigs, crSigCdPam.ConCheques)), ;
				Datas	  With TempChq.Datas,;
				DtAlts    With ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao)

		If llInsCliNoChq
			Select crSigChe
			Replace Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis , ;
					Grclis With TempChq.GrClis
				
			If crSigCdOpt.Origems # 1 And crSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'S'	
				Select crSigChe
				Replace GruOrigs With Tempchq.GrClis, ;
						ConOrigs With TempChq.Iclis
			EndIf
		EndIf
		=fCriarCurSit(CrSigChe.Iclis)
	Else
		_Num = CrCheque.Nums
		If Empty(CrCheque.Leituras) And !Empty(TempChq.Leituras)
			lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Leituras = ']+TempChq.Leituras+[' ,DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		EndIf
		If Not Empty(Tempchq.grures) And Not Empty(TempChq.ConRes)
			lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set GruRes = ']+TempChq.GruRes+[' ,ConRes = ']+TempChq.ConRes+[' Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		Endif
		=fCriarCurSit(CrCheque.Iclis)
	EndIf

	If Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1
		llVenc     = TempChq.Vencs
		lcIclis    = iif(crSigCdOpt.EmiInds ='S',TempChq.Iclis,crSigChLcq.Iclis)
		lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Vencs  = ?llVenc, Iclis = ?lcIclis Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	Endif

	If ThisForm.pcEscolha='INSERIR' And (crSigCdOpt.GeraNarqs = 1)
		_Arq=fGerUniqueKey('CHEQUEARQUIVO')
		If llInseCheq
			Select crSigChe
			Replace	crSigChe.Numeros With Trans(_Arq,'@L 999999'), CrSigChe.DtAlts With _pData
		Else
			lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Numeros = ']+Trans(_Arq,'@L 999999')+[' ,DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		Endif
		Select TempChq
		Replace	TempChq.Numeros With Trans(_Arq,'@L 999999')
	EndIf
	
	If crSigCdOpt.Origems = 1 And !Empty(crSigChLcq.GruOrigs) And !Empty(crSigChLcq.ConOrigs)
		lcUltGruDests = crSigChLcq.GruOrigs
		lcUltConDests = crSigChLcq.ConOrigs
	Else
		If llInseCheq
			lcUltGruDests = crSigChe.Grupos
			lcUltConDests = crSigChe.Contas
		Else
			lcUltGruDests = crCheque.Grupos
			lcUltConDests = crCheque.Contas
		Endif
	Endif

	lnUltValor    = TempChq.Valors
	lnUltCota     = fCarregarCambio(crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, crSigChLcq.DtEnts)
	lcUltMoeda    = crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs
	* Richard em 21/05/2012 - Vianna, pois usa cheques em Dolar
*!*		lnUltCota     = crSigChLcq.Cotas
*!*		lcUltMoeda    = crSigChLcq.Moeds

	If crSigCdOpt.EmpInds = [S] and !Empty(TempChq.Emps)
		If llInseCheq
			Select crSigChe
			Replace crSigChe.Emps With TempChq.Emps, CrSigChe.DtAlts With _pData
		Else
			lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Emps = ']+TempChq.Emps+[', DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
			lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf
		Endif
	EndIf
	
	_GrupoAnt = lcUltGruDests
	_ContaAnt = lcUltConDests
	_ValorAnt = TempChq.Valors
	_CotaAnt  = fCarregarCambio(crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, crSigChLcq.DtEnts)
	_MoedaAnt = crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs
	
	Select crSigCdOpt
	Set Order to operacao
	Seek crSigChLcq.codigos

	lcWhere = [Select * From SigCqChm Where Bancos=']+TempChq.Bancos+[' And Agencias = ']+TempChq.Agencias+[']+;
			  [And NContas=']+TempChq.nContas+['And NCheques=']+TempChq.NCheques+[' Order By Nums, DtSis Desc ]
	lnErro = Thisform.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWhere,'CrChm')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	Select CrChm
	Go Top
	
	If !Eof() And !Empty(CrChm.Numos)
		lcOldEmps    = CrChm.Emps 
		lcOldNumos   = CrChm.Numos
		lcOldCodigos = CrChm.Codigos
	EndIf

	Select crSigCqChm
	Append blank
	Replace DtEnts     With crSigChLcq.DtEnts  ;
			DtSis 	   With _pData;
			codigos	   With crSigChLcq.codigos ;
			nums       With _num     ;
			tipos      With crSigCdOpt.tipos   ;
			usuarios   With usuar ;
			numlotes   With crSigChLcq.numlotes;
			autos      With .f.   ;
			datatrans  With crSigChLcq.datatrans ;
			Emps	   With _Empr ;
			contas     With crSigChLcq.Contas  ;
			grupos     With crSigChLcq.Grupos  ;
			moeds	   With crSigChLcq.moeds   ;
			Cotas      With crSigChLcq.Cotas   ;
			valtots    With crSigChLcq.valtots ;
			valconvs   With crSigChLcq.valconvs;
			iclis	   With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.iclis,CrCheque.Iclis)   ;
			grclis     With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.grclis,CrCheque.GrClis)  ;
			bancos 	   With TempChq.bancos  ;
			agencias   With TempChq.agencias;
			ncontas	   With TempChq.ncontas ;
			ncheques   With TempChq.ncheques;
			NAlineas   With TempChq.NAlineas;
			Protocolos With TempChq.Protocolos;
			GruCarts   With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GruCarts,CrCheque.GruCarts) ;
			ConCarts   With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.ConCarts,CrCheque.ConCarts) ;
			GruOrigs   With lcUltGruDests;
			ConOrigs   With lcUltConDests ;
			CidChaves  With fUniqueIds() ;
			Hists      With TempChq.Obs ;
			Cmc7       with TempChq.Cmc7

	If !llInseCheq
		Replace ;
			iclis	   With crCheque.iclis ,;
			grclis     With crCheque.grclis
	Endif
			
	If Thisform.pcEscolha='INSERIR' And crSigCdOpt.EmiProts = 'S'
		lcProtocolos = Trans(fGerUniqueKey('PROTOCOLOCHQ'),"@L 99999999")
		Replace Protocolos With lcProtocolos
		Select TempChq
		Replace Protocolos With lcProtocolos
	Endif

	If llInseCheq
		Select crSigChe
		Replace Protocolos with TempChq.Protocolos, DtAlts With _pData
		lcNum = CrSigChe.Nums
	Else
		lcNum = CrCheque.Nums
		lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Protocolos = ']+TempChq.Protocolos+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	Endif

	If !isEmpty(TempChq.Obs)
		Select crSigCheH
		Append Blank
		Replace Nums  With lcNum,;
				Datas With ldDtEnt,;
				Hists With Nvl(TempChq.Obs, ''), ;
				autos With .t.,;
				NumLotes With lnNumLote,;
				Emps  With _Empr,;
				CidChaves With fUniqueIds()
	Endif

	_NOper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
	_VOper = _Empr + Alltrim( Str( _NOper, 9 ) ) 
	_NOper2 = _NOper
	_Dep = .f.
	_FPag = Iif(llInseCheq,CrSigChe.FPags,CrCheque.Fpags)

	If Seek( _Fpag, 'CrSigOpFp', 'Fpags' ) And crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1 And crSigCdOpt.BxPends = 'S'
		_Cheque = tempchq.bancos+tempchq.agencias+tempchq.ncontas+tempchq.ncheques
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPbx','CrSigCdPbx',@laCampo)
			Select CrSigCdPbx
			Scan For Valos # 0
				If !Deposits Or ( crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'DB' And crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S' )
					_GrupoOrig = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
					_ContaOrig = crSigCdPam.contarecs
					_GrupoDest = crSigCdPbx.Grupos
					_ContaDest = crSigCdPbx.Contas
					lcUltGruDests = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
					lcUltConDests = crSigCdPam.contarecs
					If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
						_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
								iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
						        ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
					Else
						_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
						If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
							_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
						Endif
					Endif
					_Opers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','D','C') 
					_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')
					_Cot = fCarregarCambio(crSigCdPbx.Moedas,ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao))
					_Valor = crSigCdPbx.Valos
					If _Valor < 0
						_saveoper = _opers
						_opers = _sopers
						_sopers = _saveoper
						_valor = Abs( _valor )
					Endif
					If Round( _Valor, 2 ) # 0
						pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
						Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
								docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
								sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, shists, CidChaves  ) ;
							Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TempChq.Vencs, ;
									_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigCdPbx.Moedas, _Cot, ;
									_Valor, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
									crSigCdPbx.Moedas, _Cot, _Valor, 0, 'X', pCidC)

						=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr )
						_ValPags = Iif( crSigCdPbx.Tipos = 'X', 0, Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S', 0, _Valor ) )

						pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
						Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
								docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
								sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, shists, CidChaves  ) ;
							Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), TempChq.Vencs, ;
									_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, crSigCdPbx.Moedas, _Cot, ;
									_Valor, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
									crSigCdPbx.Moedas, _Cot, _Valor, _ValPags, 'X', pCidC)
								
						=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

						_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')

					Endif
					Replace crSigCdPbx.Deposits With .t.
				EndIf
				
				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Emps=']+CrSigCdPbx.Emps+[' And Dopes=']+CrSigCdPbx.Dopes+[']+;
						 [ And Numes=]+Str(CrSigCdPbx.Numes,6)
				
				LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf
				
				Select CrPar
				Scan
					If _Cheque = bancos + agencias + contas + numeros
						lcpago = '0'
						ldDat = _pData
						
						lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
									  [Set VPags = ] + Str(CrPar.Valos,12,2) + [, ] + ;
									  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
									  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
								    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']
						
						lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
					Endif
				Endscan
				_Dep = .T.
			Endscan
		Else
			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+Tempchq.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+TempChq.Agencias+[']+;
					 [ And contas=']+TempChq.NContas+[' And numeros=']+Tempchq.Ncheques+[']

			LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf

			Select CrPar
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0 And ( crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR' And crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S' )
				lcpago = '0'
				ldDat = _pData
				
				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
							  [Set VPags = Valos, ] + ;
							  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
							  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
						    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']
						    
				lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				_GrupoOrig = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
				_ContaOrig = crSigCdPam.contarecs
				_GrupoDest = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GruOrigs,CrCheque.GruOrigs)
				_ContaDest = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.ConOrigs,CrCheque.ConOrigs)
				lcUltGruDests = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
				lcUltConDests = crSigCdPam.contarecs
				If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
					_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
							iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;					
							' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
				Else
					_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
					If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
						_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
					Endif
				Endif
				_Opers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','D','C') 
				_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')
				_Valor = TempChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas
				If _Valor < 0
					_saveoper = _opers
					_opers = _sopers
					_sopers = _saveoper
					_valor = Abs( _valor )
				Endif
				If Round( _Valor, 2 ) # 0
					pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
					Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
							docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
							sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
						Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), TempChq.Vencs, ;
								_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
								_Valor, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
								crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, tempchq.valors, _Valor, pCidc)

					=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
							
					pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
					Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
							docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
							sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves ) ;
						Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), TempChq.Vencs, ;
								_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, ;
								tempchq.valors, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
								_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, _Valor, ;
								Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S' , 0, tempchq.valors ), pCidC)

					=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

				Endif
				_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
			Endif
		Endif
	Else
		=Seek( CrChm.Codigos, 'crSigCdOpt', 'Operacao' )
		lcNumo = Padr( crChm.Emps + Alltrim( Str( crChm.Numos ) ), 10 )
		lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Vopers=?lcNumo ]
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')
		
		Select CrMccr
		Scan
			_Grupo1 = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GruOrigs + CrSigChe.ConOrigs,CrCheque.GruOrigs + CrCheque.ConOrigs)
			_Grupo2 = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.Grupos + CrSigChe.Contas,CrCheque.Grupos + CrCheque.Contas)
			If ((Grupos + Contas = _Grupo1 And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] And crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S') Or ;
				(Grupos + Contas = _Grupo2 And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] And crSigCdOpt.ValPend2s = 'S' )) And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1
				lcpago = '0'
				lcDtps = CrMccr.Dopcs + CrMccr.Opers + crmccr.tipos + lcPago
				ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = Valors, pagos=?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
			Endif
		Endscan
		Select crSigCdOpt
		=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
	Endif

	If ( Seek( _Fpag, 'crSigOpFp', 'Fpags' ) And crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1 And crSigCdOpt.BxPends # 'S' ) Or ( crSigCdOpt.FazContas = 'S' And Empty( _FPag ) )

		_GrupoOrig = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
		_ContaOrig = crSigCdPam.contarecs
		_GrupoDest = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GruOrigs,CrCheque.GruOrigs)
		_ContaDest = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.ConOrigs,Crcheque.ConOrigs)

		If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
					iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;			
					' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
		Else
			_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
				_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
			Endif
		Endif
		_Opers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','D','C') 
		_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')
		If Round( TempChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, 2 ) # 0
			pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
			Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
					docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
					sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
				Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), TempChq.Vencs, ;
						_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
						TempChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
						_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, tempchq.valors, 0, pCidc)

			=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

			lnValPends2 = tempchq.valors/crSigChLcq.Cotas
			If Empty(crSigCdOpt.FPags)
				If crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S'
					lnValPends2 = 0
				EndIf
			Else
				If crSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1
					lnValPends2 = 0
				EndIf
			EndIf
			
			pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+Thisform.Sigkey 
			Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
					docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
					sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
				Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), TempChq.Vencs, ;
						_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, ;
						tempchq.valors, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
						_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, TempChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
						lnValPends2, pCidc)

			=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

			_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')

		Endif

		_GrupoAnt = _GrupoOrig
		_ContaAnt = _ContaOrig
		_ValorAnt = TempChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas
		_CotaAnt  = crSigChLcq.Cotas
		_MoedaAnt = crSigChLcq.Moeds

	Endif
***********************************************************************************************
	If Not Empty( _FPag ) And Seek( _FPag, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' ) And ;
			( crSigOpFp.BxComChqs = 1 ) And ( crSigCdOpt.BxPends = 'S' )
		Create Cursor TmpComiss ( Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Valos n(12,2), Moedas c(3), Nopers n(6), Cotacaos n(12,4), Contas c(10))
		Index on Emps+dopes+Str(Numes,6)+moedas+str(nopers,9) Tag Empdopnum

		If llInseCheq
			_Cheque = crSigChe.Bancos + crSigChe.Agencias + crSigChe.NContas + crSigChe.NCheques
		Else
			_Cheque = crCheque.Bancos + crCheque.Agencias + crCheque.NContas + crCheque.NCheques
		Endif
		
		lcWher = [Select * From SigCdPbx Where Bancos=']+Tempchq.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+Tempchq.Agencias+[' ]+;
				 [And NContas=']+TempChq.Ncontas+[' And Ncheques=']+TempChq.NCheques+[']

		LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPrBx')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		
		Select CrPrBx
		Go top
		If Reccount() > 0
			Scan For Valos # 0
				laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
				laCampo3(1,2)=CrPrbx.Nopers
				laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
				laCampo3(2,2)=CrPrBx.Empos

				If CrPrbx.Tipos = "P" And ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar',@lacampo3)
					Select CrPar
					Go Top
					If !Seek( CrPar.Emps + CrPar.Dopes + Str( CrPar.Numes, 6 ) + CrPrbx.Moedas + Str( CrPrbx.Nopers, 7 ), 'TmpComiss' )
						Insert Into TmpComiss ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moedas, Nopers, Cotacaos, Contas  ) ;
								Values ( CrPar.Emps, CrPar.Dopes, CrPar.Numes, CrPrbx.Moedas, CrPrbx.Nopers, CrPrbx.Cotacaos, CrPrbx.Contas )
					Endif
					Replace TmpComiss.Valos With TmpComiss.Valos + CrPrbx.Valos
				Endif
				If CrPrbx.Tipocs = "P"
					If !Seek( CrPrbx.Emps + CrPrbx.Dopecs + Str( CrPrbx.Numecs, 6 ) + CrPrbx.Moecs + Str( CrPrbx.Nopercs, 7 ), 'TmpComiss' )
						Insert Into TmpComiss ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Moedas, Nopers, Cotacaos, Contas ) ;
								Values ( CrPrbx.Emps, CrPrbx.Dopecs, CrPrbx.Numecs, CrPrbx.Moecs, CrPrbx.Nopercs, CrPrbx.Cotacaocs, CrPrbx.Contas )
					Endif
					Replace TmpComiss.Valos With TmpComiss.Valos + CrPrbx.Valocs
				Endif
			EndScan
			
			lcDadChq = 'Ch:'+Tempchq.Bancos+'-'+Tempchq.Agencias+'-'+TempChq.NCheques
			
			Select TmpComiss
			Scan for TmpComiss.Valos # 0 And !Empty( TmpComiss.Moedas )
				_ValBaixa = TmpComiss.Valos
				_ChaveEest = TmpComiss.Emps + TmpComiss.Dopes + Str( TmpComiss.Numes, 6 )

				laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
				laCampo3(1,2)=TmpComiss.Nopers
				laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'
				laCampo3(2,2)=TmpComiss.Emps

				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',_ChaveEest)
				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',TmpComiss.dopes)
				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvPar','CrPar',@laCampo3)
				
				Select CrEest
				Go Top
				Select CrOpe
				Go top
				Select CrPar
				Go Top
				
				_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
				If crOpe.CMoes # Tmpcomiss.Moedas
					_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa * crPar.CotFpgs
					If Empty( crOpe.CMoes )
						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
					Else
						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
						_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
					Endif
				Endif
				_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera
				
				lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCmm Where Emps=']+TmpComiss.emps+[' And Dopes=']+TmpComiss.Dopes+[' ]+;
						 [And Numes=]+Str(TmpComiss.Numes,6)
				
				LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCmm')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				Select CrSigCdCmm
				Scan
					_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
					Replace crSigCdCmm.Baixas With crSigCdCmm.Baixas + ;
												 Iif(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR',1,-1) * _Baixar
					If _Baixar # 0
						lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrSigCdCmm.Baixas,12,2)+;
								   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrSigCdCmm.CidChaves+[']
						LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
						lcMoeCom = ' '
						If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'						
							xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
							xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
							_Hist = Alltrim( CrEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( CrEest.Mascnum ) + ' '
							_Hist2 = ''
						Else
							xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
							xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
							_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
							        iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
									' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'
							_Hist2 = ''
							
						Endif
						If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists ) 
							If Empty(_Hist)
								If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'
									_Hist = crEest.Emps + Alltrim( crEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( crEest.Mascnum ) + ' - Comissão'
								Else
									_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
											iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
											' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'
								EndIf
							Endif
						Else
							_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
							If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
								_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
							Endif
						Endif
						lcSql = [Select ComMoedas From SigCdGcr Where Codigos = ']+CrSigCdCmm.Grupos+[']
						If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpGccr') < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão Inserir(TmpGccr)')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
						If Not Empty(TmpGccr.ComMoedas) And TmpGccr.ComMoedas # CrSigCdCmm.Moedas
							lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
							lnCota = fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
							lnMoeda = TmpGccr.ComMoedas
						Else
							Go Top in crSigCdPam
							If crSigCdPam.ComMoedas # crSigCdCmm.Moedas And !Empty( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas )
								lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnCota = fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnMoeda = crSigCdPam.ComMoedas
							Else
								lnValor = _Baixar
								lnCota = 1
								lnMoeda = crSigCdCmm.Moedas
							EndIf
						Endif
						Select TmpMccr
						If !Seek(crSigCdCmm.Grupos+crSigCdCmm.Contas+Padr(_Hist,40)+Padr(lnMoeda,3)+Padr(lcMoeCom,3)+Padr(_Hist2,40))
							_noper2 = fGerUniqueKey( 'SigMvCcr' )
							Append Blank
							Replace NOpers   With _noper2,;
									VOpers   With _voper,;
									Emps     With _Empr,;
									Datas    With crSigChLcq.DtEnts,;
									usualts  With usuar,;
									datalts  With _pData,;
									Vencs    With _pData,;
									hists    With _hist,;
									opers    With xoper,;
									sopers   With xsoper,;
									moedas   With lnMoeda,;
									cotacaos With lnCota,;
									autos    With .t.,;
									grupos   With crSigCdCmm.Grupos,;
									contas   With crSigCdCmm.Contas,;
									sgrupos  With crSigCdPam.ComGrupos,;
									scontas  With crSigCdPam.ComContas,;
									smoedas  With lnMoeda,;
									scotacaos With lnCota,;
									Hist2s   With _Hist2,;
									Tipos    With 'X',;
									MoeComs  With lcMoeCom
						Endif
						Replace valors  With Valors + lnValor,;
								svalors With Valors
					Endif
				Endscan
			Endscan
		Else
			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+TempChq.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+TempChq.Agencias+[']+;
					 [ And contas=']+TempChq.NContas+[' And numeros=']+TempChq.Ncheques+[']

			LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
			IF lnErro < 1
				MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
				Return .f.
			EndIf

			Select CrPar
			Go Top
			If Reccount() > 0
				_ValBaixa = crPar.Valos
				_ChaveEest = crPar.Emps + crPar.Dopes + Str( crPar.Numes, 6 )

				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',_ChaveEest)
				=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',CrPar.dopes)
				
				Select CrEest
				Go Top
				Select CrOpe
				Go top
				
				Select CrPar

				_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
				If crOpe.CMoes # Moefpgs
					_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa * crPar.Cotfpgs
					If Empty( crOpe.CMoes )
						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
					Else
						_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
						_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
					Endif
				Endif
				_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera

				lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCmm Where Emps=']+CrPar.emps+[' And Dopes=']+crPar.Dopes+[' ]+;
						 [And Numes=]+Str(crpar.Numes,6)
				
				LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCmm')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				Select CrSigCdCmm
				Scan
					_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
					Replace crSigCdCmm.Baixas With crSigCdCmm.Baixas + ;
												 IIf(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR',1,-1) * _Baixar
					lcMoeCom = crSigCdCmm.Moedas
					If _Baixar # 0

						lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrSigCdCmm.Baixas,12,2)+;
								   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrSigCdCmm.CidChaves+[']
						LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf

						If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'						
							xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
							xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
						Else
							xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
							xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
						Endif
						If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
							If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'						
								_Hist = crEest.Emps + Alltrim( crEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( crEest.Mascnum ) + ' - Comissão'
							Else
								_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
										iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
										' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'
							Endif
						Else
							_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
							If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
								_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
							Endif
						Endif
						_Hist2 = ''
						
						lcSql = [Select ComMoedas From SigCdGcr Where Codigos = ']+CrSigCdCmm.Grupos+[']
						If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpGccr') < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão Inserir(TmpGccr)')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
						If Not Empty(TmpGccr.ComMoedas) And TmpGccr.ComMoedas # CrSigCdCmm.Moedas
							lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
							lnCota = fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
							lnMoeda = TmpGccr.ComMoedas
						Else
							Go Top in crSigCdPam
							If crSigCdPam.ComMoedas # crSigCdCmm.Moedas And !Empty( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas )
								lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnCota = fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnMoeda = crSigCdPam.ComMoedas
							Else
								lnValor = _Baixar
								lnCota = 1
								lnMoeda = crSigCdCmm.Moedas
							EndIf
						EndIf
						Select TmpMccr
						If !Seek(crSigCdCmm.Grupos+crSigCdCmm.Contas+Padr(_Hist,40)+Padr(lnMoeda,3)+Padr(lcMoeCom,3)+Padr(_Hist2,40))
							Append Blank
							Replace NOpers   With _noper2,;
									VOpers   With _voper,;
									Emps     With _Empr,;
									Datas    With _pData,;
									usualts  With usuar,;
									datalts  With _pData,;
									Vencs    With _pData,;
									hists    With _hist,;
									opers    With xoper,;
									sopers   With xsoper,;
									moedas   With lnMoeda,;
									cotacaos With lnCota,;
									autos    With .t.,;
									grupos   With crSigCdCmm.Grupos,;
									contas   With crSigCdCmm.Contas,;
									sgrupos  With crSigCdPam.ComGrupos,;
									scontas  With crSigCdPam.ComContas,;
									smoedas  With lnMoeda,;
									scotacaos With lnCota,;
									Hist2s   With _Hist2,;
									Tipos    With 'X',;
									MoeComs  With lcMoeCom
						Endif
						Replace valors  With Valors + lnValor,;
								svalors With Valors
						_Noper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
					Endif
				Endscan
			EndIf
		EndIf		
	EndIf
	
	_TotalMov = _TotalMov + TempChq.Valors
			
	Select crSigCqChm
	Replace numos with _NOper
	If llInseCheq
		Select crSigChe
		Replace numos with _NOper, DtAlts With _pData
		laCampo4(1,1)='Grupos'
		laCampo4(1,2)=CrSigChe.GrClis
		laCampo4(2,1)='Contas'
		laCampo4(2,2)=CrSigChe.Iclis
		laCampo4(3,1)='Cocos'
		laCampo4(3,2)=CrSigCdOpt.CCentrals
	Else
		lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Numos = ]+Str(_Noper)+[ , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
		laCampo4(1,1)='Grupos'
		laCampo4(1,2)=CrCheque.GrClis
		laCampo4(2,1)='Contas'
		laCampo4(2,2)=CrCheque.Iclis
		laCampo4(3,1)='Cocos'
		laCampo4(3,2)=CrSigCdOpt.CCentrals
	Endif
	If !Empty(crSigCdOpt.Ccentrals)
		If !ThisForm.podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigClLan',@laCampo4)
			Select CrSigClLan
			Append Blank
			Replace Grupos With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GrClis,CrCheque.GrClis),;
					Contas With Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.Iclis,CrCheque.Iclis),;
					Cocos  With crSigCdOpt.Ccentrals,;
					CidChaves With fUniqueIds(),;
					GruContas With Grupos + Contas
		Endif
	Endif

	If crSigCdOpt.HistClis = 'S'	&& Lança Histórico do cliente
		If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			lcHistClis = crSigChLcq.Emps + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
						 iif(Empty(TempChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TempChq.NAlineas))) + ;
						 ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
		Else
			lcHistClis = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
				lcHistClis = lcHistClis + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
			Endif
		Endif
		lcHistClis = lcHistClis + ' (' + TempChq.Bancos + '-' + TempChq.Agencias + '-' + ;
                     TempChq.Ncontas+ '-' + TempChq.NCheques + ' ' + ;
                     AllTrim(Transf(TempChq.Valors,'999,999,999.99')) + ')'
		
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrCli','Iclis',CrSigChe.Iclis)
			Select CrCli
			Go Top
			lcGruCliH = Evaluate('crCli.Grupos')
		Else
			lcGruCliH = crSigChe.GrClis
		EndIf			

		If !Empty(lcHistClis)
			Insert Into crSigCdClh (Autos, Grupos, Iclis, Datas, Hists, Emps, Nopers, CidChaves, GruContas ) Values ;
								(.T., lcGruCliH, Iif(llInseCheq,CrSigChe.Iclis,CrCheque.Iclis), CtoT(DtoC(crSigChLcq.DtEnts)), ;
								lcHistClis, crSigCqChm.Emps, crSigCqChm.Numos, fUniqueIds(), lcGruCliH + Iif(llInseCheq,CrSigChe.Iclis,CrCheque.Iclis)) 
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	If CrSigCdOpt.Destinos = 2 And CrSigCdOpt.tipos='DB' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens > 1
		_GrupoDest = Iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.GrClis,CrCheque.GrClis)
		_ContaDest = iif(llInseCheq,crSigChe.Iclis,CrCheque.Iclis)
		Select CrSigCqChm
		Replace Grupos With _GrupoDest,;
				Contas With _ContaDest
	Else
		_GrupoDest = crSigChLcq.Grupos
		_ContaDest = crSigChLcq.Contas
	EndIf
			
	lcProcura = lcUltGruDests + lcUltConDests + lcUltMoeda + _GrupoDest + _ContaDest
	
	If !Seek(lcProcura,'TmpConsolida','TGruConMoe') Or (crSigCdOpt.Consolidas = 'N')
		Insert Into TmpConsolida (TGrupos, TContas, TMoedas, TValors, TCotas, oGrupos, oContas) ;
			Values (lcUltGruDests, lcUltConDests, lcUltMoeda, lnUltValor, lnUltCota, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest)
	Else
		Select TmpConsolida
		Replace TValors With TValors + lnUltValor
	EndIf

	If llInseCheq
		Select crSigChe
		Replace	;
