			crSigChe.Nalineas With IIF(TempChq.Nalineas>0,TempChq.nAlineas,CrSigChe.nAlineas) ;
			crSigChe.umovs 	  With crSigChLcq.codigos;
			crSigChe.Contas   With crSigChLcq.Contas ;
			crSigChe.Grupos   With crSigChLcq.Grupos ;
			crSigChe.DtAlts   With _pData
	Else
		lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set ]+IIF(TempChq.nAlineas>0,[Nalineas=]+Str(TempChq.Nalineas)+[, ],[])+[ Umovs=']+CrSigChLcq.Codigos+[' ]+;
				   [, Contas=']+CrSigChLcq.contas+[' , Grupos=']+CrSigChLcq.Grupos+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf
	Endif
	
	&& Baixar Pendencias da Movimentação Anterior do Cheque
	&& Quando um cheque vem de Troca de Cheques, e está configurado como valor pendente,
	&& a próxima movimentação desse cheque precisa preencher SigMvCcr.ValPags
	If !Empty(lcOldNumos)
		lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Emps=']+lcOldEmps+[' And Nopers=]+Str(lcOldNumos)
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')

		Select crMccr
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0 And !Empty(CrMccr.Vopers)
			=Seek(lcOldCodigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
			lcOldVOper = crMccr.VOpers
			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where VOpers=']+lcOldVoper+[']
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrvMccr')
			Select CrvMccr
			Scan
				If ( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = crSigChe.GruOrigs + crSigChe.ConOrigs And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] ) Or ;
					( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = lcOldGrupos + lcOldContas And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] )
					lcpago = '0'
					lcDtps = CrvMccr.Dopcs + CrvMccr.Opers + CrvMccr.Tipos + lcPago
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = Valors, pagos = ?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrvMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
				Endif
			Endscan
		EndIf
	Endif

	=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
	Select TempChq
EndScan

Select TmpMccr
Index on Nopers Tag Nopers
Scan
	Scatter to Memvar
	Select crSigMvCcr
	Append From Array Memvar
	Replace CrSigMvCcr.cidchaves  With Dtos(datas)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(Datas)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey ,;
			CrSigMvCcr.gruconmoes with CrSigMvCcr.grupos + CrSigMvCcr.contas + CrSigMvCcr.moedas
	
	=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

	ThisForm.FazContra()

EndScan

If _TotalMov # 0
	Select TmpConsolida
	Scan
		_GrupoOrig = TmpConsolida.TGrupos
		_ContaOrig = TmpConsolida.TContas
		_MoedaOrig = TmpConsolida.TMoedas
		_CotaOrig  = TmpConsolida.TCotas
		_ValorOrig = TmpConsolida.TValors

		_GrupoDest = TmpConsolida.oGrupos &&crSigChLcq.Grupos
		_ContaDest = TmpConsolida.oContas &&crSigChLcq.Contas
		_MoedaDest = crSigChLcq.Moeds
		_CotaDest  = crSigChLcq.Cotas
		_ValorDest = _ValorOrig * _CotaOrig / _CotaDest
		
		*--
		_contems = crSigChLcq.contems
		_grupems = crSigChLcq.grupems
		_GrupoCCs = crSigChLcq.GrupoCCs
		_ContaCCs = crSigChLcq.ContaCCs
		
		_Opers     = 'C' 
		_Sopers    = 'D'
		If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
		Else
			_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
			If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
				_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
			Endif
		Endif
		
		_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
		pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+Thisform.Sigkey 
		Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
				docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
				sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves, grupems, contems ) ;
			Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), ;
					_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, ;
					_MoedaOrig, _CotaOrig, _ValorOrig, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
					_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, _MoedaDest, _CotaDest, _ValorDest, ;
					Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPend2s='S', 0, _ValorOrig ), pcidc, _grupems, _contems)

		=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				
		pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.sigkey 
		Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
				docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
				sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves, grupems, contems ) ;
			Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao), ;
					_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, ;
					_MoedaDest, _CotaDest, _ValorDest, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
					_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _MoedaOrig, _CotaOrig, _ValorOrig, 0, pCidc, _GrupoCCs, _ContaCCs)

		=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
		
		Select TmpConsolida
	EndScan
Endif

If crSigCdOpt.Parcelas = 'S'
	_Grupo = crSigChLcq.Grclis
	_Conta = crSigChLcq.Iclis
	COper  = iif( crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR', "E", "S" )
	ThisForm.ContaPar = 0
	nrlotchq = .f.
	nrlotcar = .f.
	valtot = 0
	Select xPar
	Sum Valos to ValTot For !Empty(Fpags)

	vLote = crSigChLcq.NumLoteFPs
	select xpar
	goto top
	cabec=.t.
	valtotchq = 0
	
	Scan
		If !Empty( xPar.FPags )
			ThisForm.ContaPar = ThisForm.ContaPar + 1
			select crSigOpFp
			set order to fpags
			Seek xpar.fpags
			If crSigOpFp.ValPends # 1
				Replace xPar.VPags With xPar.Valos
			Endif
			If crSigOpFp.Infos = 'C' And Empty(vLote)
				vlote = fGerUniqueKey('LOTECH')
				lnCotas = fCarregarCambio(crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs,ldDtEnt)
				Select crSigChLcq
				Replace NumLoteFPs with vLote
				Insert Into crSigChLcq (dtents, dtsis, codigos, tipos, usuarios, numlotes, emps, ;
						 moeds, Cotas, autos, Grclis, Iclis, GruCarts, ConCarts ) ;
					values (ldDtEnt, _pData,lcOpEntradas, "DB", Usuar, vLote, _Empr, ;
							crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, lnCotas, .T., crSigChLcq.GruOrigs, ;
							crSigChLcq.ConOrigs, crSigCdPam.GruCarts, crSigCdPam.ConCarts )
				Set Order to NumLotes							
				Seek (lnNumLote)
			Endif

			select crSigMvPar
			Insert Into crSigMvPar(Parcs, agencias,bancos,cartaos,contas,datas,datatrans,;
				dpags,ec_numes,emps,fpags,numeros,numes,outros,;
				valos,moefpgs,cotfpgs,cotusus,vencs,vpags,ncarnes,;
				nrecs,nocreditos,dtdeps,numchqs,ncarts,impcars,dcarts,cotusus,obs,;
				cnNsuParcs,cnIdTefs, NumLoteChs, CidChaves, Pagos, DtAlts ) ;
				values (xPar.Parcs, xpar.agencias, xpar.bancos, xpar.cartaos, xpar.Contas, xpar.datas, ;
				xpar.datatrans, xpar.dpags, xpar.ec_numes, crSigChLcq.Emps, ;
				xpar.fpags, xpar.numeros, 0, xpar.outros, xpar.valos, xpar.moefpgs, ;
				xpar.cotfpgs, xpar.cotusus, xpar.vencs, xpar.vpags, xpar.ncarnes, ;
			    xpar.nrecs, xpar.nocreditos, xpar.dtdeps, xpar.numchqs, xpar.ncarts, ;
			    xpar.impcars, xpar.dcarts, xpar.cotusus, xpar.obs,;
			    xPar.cnNsuParcs,xPar.cnIdTefs, lnNumLote, fUniqueIds(), ;
			    Iif(crSigMvPar.Valos=CrSigMvPar.Vpags,'0','1'), _pData )
		
			If coper = "E" 
				select crSigOpFp
				set order to fpags
				Seek xpar.fpags
				select xpar
				*- Grava cheques -*
				If crSigOpFp.infos = "C"  and !Empty(bancos)
					valtotchq = valtotchq + xpar.valos
					laCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
					laCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
					laCampo(3,1)='ncontas'
					laCampo(4,1)='ncheques'
					laCampo(1,2)=Tempchq.Bancos
					laCampo(2,2)=Tempchq.Agencias
					laCampo(3,2)=Tempchq.ncontas
					laCampo(4,2)=Tempchq.ncheques
					
					llInseCheq = .f.
					
					If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@laCampo)
						_Num = CrCheque.Nums
						lcUpdate = [Update SigChe Set Umovs=']+lcOpEntradas+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt ]+;
								   [Where Nums = ']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
						lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
						IF lnErro < 1
							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf
					Else
						_num=trans(fGerUniqueKey('busnum'),"@L 99999999")
						Select crSigChe
						Insert Into crSigChe ( Bancos, Agencias,NContas, NCheques, Valors, Vencs, ;
								Nums, Umovs, Iclis, GrClis, Autos, DataTrans, Emps, Notas, ;
								FPags, GruOrigs, ConOrigs, GruCarts, ConCarts, Grupos, Contas, ;
								Datas, DtAlts ) ;
							values ( xPar.Bancos, xPar.Agencias, xPar.Contas, xPar.Numeros, ;
								xPar.Valos, xPar.Vencs, _Num, lcOpEntradas, crSigChLcq.Iclis, ;
								crSigChLcq.Grclis, .T., xPar.DataTrans, crSigChLcq.Emps, '', ;
								xPar.FPags, crSigChLcq.GruOrigs, crSigChLcq.ConOrigs, ;
								crSigCdPam.GruCarts, crSigCdPam.ConCarts, crSigOpFp.GrupoDs, ;
								crSigOpFp.ContaDs, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, _pData )
					EndIf
						
					Insert Into crSigCqChm (DtEnts, DtSis, codigos, moeds, Cotas,;
							valtots,valconvs,nums,tipos,usuarios,numlotes,emps,;
							autos,datatrans,grclis,iclis,bancos,agencias,ncontas,ncheques, ;
							GruOrigs, ConOrigs, GruCarts, ConCarts, Grupos, Contas, Hists, CidChaves) ;
						values (crSigChLcq.DtEnts, _pData, lcOpEntradas, xpar.moefpgs, 1, ;
							xpar.valos, xpar.valos,_num, "DB", usuar, vlote, crSigChLcq.Emps,;
							.t.,xpar.datatrans,crSigChLcq.Grclis, crSigChLcq.Iclis,;
							xpar.bancos,xpar.agencias,xpar.Contas,xpar.numeros, ;
							crSigChLcq.GruOrigs, crSigChLcq.ConOrigs, crSigCdPam.GruCarts, ;
							crSigCdPam.ConCarts, crSigOpFp.GrupoDs, crSigOpFp.ContaDs, xPar.Obs, fUniqueIds())

					If !isEmpty(xPar.Obs)
						Select crSigCheH
						Append Blank
						Replace Nums  With _Num,;
								Datas With crSigChLcq.DtEnts,;
								Hists With Left(xpar.Obs,76),;
								autos With .t.,;
								NumLotes With vLote,;
								Emps  With _Empr,;
								CidChaves With fUniqueIds()
					Endif
				EndIf
				*- Grava cartao de credito -*
				If crSigOpFp.infos = "T" and !Empty(xpar.nocreditos)
					*- Nr. lote e nr cartao -*
					nlotec = str(fGerUniqueKey('nlotecs'),10)
					ncart = fGerUniqueKey('ncarts')
					select crSigCdCar
					Insert Into crSigCdCar (Emps, Datas, FPags, nlotecs, iclis, NumLoteChs);
							values (crSigChLcq.Emps, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, xPar.DCarts, nlotec, ;
							        _CONTA, lnNumLote)

					Insert Into crSigCdCtt (Parcs, emps, datas, datatrans,	cartaos, nocreditos, ;
										 fpags, valos, ncarts, nlotecs, notas, taxas, iclis, ;
										 NumLoteChs) ;
					values (xPar.Parcs, crSigChLcq.Emps, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, crSigChLcq.DataTrans,;
						xpar.cartaos, xpar.nocreditos, xpar.dcarts, xpar.valos, ;
						ncart,nlotec,'',crSigOpFp.desc2s,_CONTA, lnNumLote)
						
					If crSigOpFp.carauts='S'
						select crSigCdCrd
						Insert Into crSigCdCrd (Parcs, dt_deposit,datatrans,fpags,;
							noresumos,recebidos,valos,totres,vencs,vr_recebs,;
							emps,cartaos,nocreditos,datas,parcs,;
							ctccs,ccons,nmcos,taxas,ncarts,notas,nlotecs,iclis, NumLoteChs );
						values (xPar.Parcs, xpar.vencs,crSigCdCtt.datatrans,crSigCdCtt.fpags,;
							crSigCdCtt.nocreditos,xpar.vencs,xpar.VALOS,xpar.VALOS,xpar.vencs,xpar.valos,;
							crSigCdCtt.emps,crSigCdCtt.cartaos,crSigCdCtt.nocreditos,crSigCdCtt.datas,crSigCdCtt.parcs,;
							crSigOpFp.grupods,crSigOpFp.contads,crSigCdCtt.nmcos,crSigCdCtt.taxas,crSigCdCtt.ncarts,crSigCdCtt.notas,;
							nlotec,_CONTA, lnNumLote)
						
						select crSigCdCtt
						replace noresumos  with xpar.nocreditos ;
								vencs      with xpar.vencs ;
								dt_deposit with xpar.vencs ;
								ctccs      with crSigOpFp.grupods ;
								ccons      with crSigOpFp.contads 
						EndIf
					select crSigMvPar
					replace ncarts with ncart, DtAlts With _pData
				EndIf
				If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'
					If crSigOpFp.infos = "C"  and !Empty( xpar.bancos )
						lnVOper = thisform.contacorrente( .t. )
						Select crSigCqChm
						Replace crSigCqChm.Numos with lnVOper
					Else
						Thisform.ContaCorrente()
					Endif
				Endif
			EndIf
		Endif
		select xpar
	endscan
	
	If vLote # 0
		Select crSigChLcq
		Set Order to NumLotes
		Seek (vLote)
		
		If !Eof('crSigChLcq')
			If Valtotchq # 0
				Replace ValTots With Valtotchq, ;
					    ValConvs With Valtotchq
			Else
				Delete
			EndIf
		Endif
	EndIf
EndIf

If _TotalMov # CrSigChLcq.ValTots
	MessageBox('Diferença nos totais de cheques !!!',0+16)
	Return .f.
Endif

Select crSigChLcq
Set Order to NumLotes
Seek (lnNumLote)

Zap In TmpConsolida
IF !ThisForm.Negativar()
	Return .f.
EndIf

Select CrSigMvCcr
Replace All GruConMoes With Grupos + Contas + Moedas,;
			Pagos      With Iif(Valors=Valpags,'0','1'),;
			EmpDopncs  WITH Emps + Dopcs + STR(numcs,6),;
			DopoTps    with Dopcs + Opers + Tipos + Pagos

llErro01 = ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCdCli')
If Not llErro01
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCdCli)')
	Select CrSigCdCli
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
Endif
llErro02 = llErro01 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigChe')
If Not llErro02
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigChe)')
	Select CrSigChe
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro03 = llErro02 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCqChm')
If Not llErro03
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCqChm)')
	Select CrSigCqChm
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro04 = llErro03 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigMvCcr')
If Not llErro04
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigMvCcr)')
	Select CrSigMvCcr
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro05 = llErro04 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigClLan')
If Not llErro05
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigClLan)')
	Select CrSigClLan
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro06 = llErro05 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCdClh')
If Not llErro06
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCdClh)')
	Select CrSigCdClh
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro07 = llErro06 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigMvPar')
If Not llErro07
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigMvPar)')
	Select CrSigMvPar
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro08 = llErro07 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCdCar')
If Not llErro08
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCdCar)')
	Select CrSigCdCar
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro09 = llErro08 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCdCtt')
If Not llErro09
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCdCtt)')
	Select CrSigCdCtt
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro10 = llErro09 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigCdCrd')
If Not llErro10
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCdCrd)')
	Select CrSigCdCrd
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro11 = llErro10 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.Update('CrSigChLcq')
If Not llErro11
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigChLcq)')
	Select CrSigChLcq
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf
llErro12 = llErro11 And ThisForm.Podatamgr.UpDate('CrSigCheh')
If Not llErro12
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão update (CrSigCheh)')
	Select CrSigCheh
	Copy To c:\erro
	Return .F.
EndIf

IF llErro01 And llErro02 And llErro03 And llErro04 And llErro05 And ;
   llErro06 And llErro07 And llErro08 And llErro09 And llErro10 And llErro11 and llErro12
	RETURN .t.
Else
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return .f.
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE limpa_che
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE limpa_che
Local lnRecNo, lnOldRecNo

Select TempChq
lnRecNo = RecNo()

Replace ;
	TempChq.Leituras With Space(34), ;
	TempChq.Bancos   With Space(3),  ;
	TempChq.Agencias With Space(4),  ;
	TempChq.NContas  With Space(10), ;
	TempChq.NCheques With Space(6),  ;
	TempChq.Vencs    With {},        ;
	TempChq.Valors   With 0,         ;
	TempChq.Valpags  With 0,		 ;
	TempChq.Datas    With {}

If CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And !Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis)
	lcCliente = crSigChLcq.Iclis
	lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
	=Thisform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
	Select CrTmpCli
	Go Top
	Select TempChq
	Replace Grclis With crtmpCli.Grupos, ;
			Iclis  With crSigChLcq.Iclis, ;
			Cpfs   With crtmpCli.Cpfs, ;
			Nomes  With crtmpCli.RClis
Else
	Select TempChq
	Replace TempChq.Grclis   With Space(10), ;
			TempChq.IClis    With Space(10), ;
			TempChq.Nomes    With Space(50), ;
			TempChq.Cpfs     With Space(20)
EndIf
	
Set Order to Cheque
Seek Space(23)
If !Eof()
	Skip
	If !Eof() And Empty(Bancos+Agencias+Ncontas+NCheques)
		lnOldRecNo = lnRecNo
		lnRecNo = Recno()
		Goto (lnOldRecNo)
		Delete
	EndIf
EndIf

Set Order to
Goto (lnRecNo)

ThisForm.RefreshCnts()	

Select TempChq

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE montagrade
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE montagrade
LOCAL lcGruFil, lcConFil, lcClasEmp, lcGruDes, lcConDes

Private ldDatai, ldDataF
Set Step On 
*** mena - 09/04/2017 - checa filtro para habilitar ou não marcacao na grade de cheques ***
IF ThisForm.pcEscolha='INSERIR' AND crSigCdOpt.ordens <> 1

	lcGruDes = ALLTRIM(thisform.pagina.dados.cntDestino.get_Grupo.Value)
	lcConDes = ALLTRIM(thisform.pagina.dados.cntDestino.get_Conta.Value)
	lcGrufil = ALLTRIM(thisform.pagina.dados.cntorigem.get_GruOrig.Value)
	lcConfil = ALLTRIM(thisform.pagina.dados.cntOrigem.get_ConOrig.Value)
	lcClasEmp= ALLTRIM(thisform.pagina.dados.cntfiltro.get_Clasemp.Value)
	ldDatai =  thisform.pagina.dados.cntFiltro.get_Datai.Value
	ldDatai =  IIF(EMPTY(ldDatai), ldDatai, fDtoSQL(ldDatai))
	ldDataf =  thisform.pagina.dados.cntFiltro.get_Dataf.Value
	ldDataf =  IIF(EMPTY(ldDataf), ldDataf, fDtoSQL(ldDataf))
	
	lcSql = [select a.* from sigche a where a.umovs in (select b.operacaos from sigcdopt b ] +;
			[where b.ordens  = ]+ALLTRIM(STR(crSigCdOpt.ordens-1))+[ and b.tipos <> ']+crSigCdOpt.tipos+[') ] +;
			IIF(EMPTY(lcGruFil),[],[ and grupos = ']+lcGrufil+[' ]) +;
			IIF(EMPTY(lcConFil),[],[ and contas = ']+lcConfil+[' ]) +;
			IIF(EMPTY(lcClasEmp),[],[ and emps in (select cemps from sigcdemp where class = ']+lcClasEmp+[') ]) +;
			IIF(EMPTY(ldDatai) or EMPTY(ldDataf),  [],[ and vencs between ?ldDatai and ?ldDataf]) +;
			[ order by bancos,agencias,ncontas,ncheques ]
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrAuxChe')
	SELECT tempchq
	ZAP

	Select CrAuxChe
	Go Top
	SCAN
		SCATTER memvar
		SELECT tempchq
		APPEND BLANK
		GATHER memvar
		replace marca with 0 in tempchq
		Select CrAuxChe
	ENDSCAN

	IF RECCOUNT('tempchq') <> 0
		thisform.pagina.dados.cntCheque.Visible = .F. 
		thisform.pagina.dados.cntFiltro.Visible = .T. 
		thisform.pagina.dados.cntFiltro.Enabled = .T. 
		thisform.pagina.dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.marca.Enabled=.T.
		thisform.pagina.dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.marca.ReadOnly  =.F.
	ELSE
		SELECT tempchq
		APPEND BLANK 
		thisform.pagina.dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.marca.Enabled = .F.
		thisform.pagina.dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.marca.ReadOnly =.T.
	ENDIF
ENDIF

thisform.pagina.dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.Refresh
		

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE negativar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE negativar
Local _TotalMov, _GrupoAnt, _ContaAnt, _CotaAnt, _MoedaAnt, _ValorAnt, lcHistClis, lcGruCliH
Local lcProtocolos, llNegativar, lnNumLote, lcCodigo, lcNumLoteAtual
Local lcOldNumos, lcOldCodigos, lcOldGrupos, lcOldContas, lcOldemps, _pData

Declare LaCampo5(4,2), laCampo3(2,2)

_pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()

Select crSigChLcq
lcNumLoteAtual = crSigChLcq.NumLotes

Create Cursor TmpConsolida ( TGrupos C(10), TContas C(10), ;
							 TMoedas C(3), TValors N(12,2), TCotas N(12,4) )
Index On TGrupos+TContas+TMoedas Tag TGruConMoe

Select a.*, b.OpAutos as Umovs From TempChq a, crSigCdAli b ;
	Where a.NAlineas = b.Codigos .and. !Empty(b.OpAutos) And ;
		  Not Empty(a.Bancos + a.Agencias + a.Ncontas + a.NCheques) ;
	Into Cursor TmpChq Order By b.OpAutos

Store '' to lcCodigo 
Store 0  to _TotalMov
Store 0  to vVal
Store 0  to _TotalMov

Select TmpChq
Scan
	If lcCodigo # TmpChq.Umovs
		If !Empty(lcCodigo)
			Select crSigChLcq
			Replace ValTots With vVal
		
			If _TotalMov # 0
				Select TmpConsolida
				Scan
					_GrupoDest = TmpConsolida.TGrupos
					_ContaDest = TmpConsolida.TContas
					_GrupoOrig = crSigChLcq.Grupos
					_ContaOrig = crSigChLcq.Contas
					_CotaPad   = TmpConsolida.TCotas
					_Opers     = 'D'
					_SOpers    = 'C'

					If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
						_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
					Else
						_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
						If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
							_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
						Endif
					Endif
					
					_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
					pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
					Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
							docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
							sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
						Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
								_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
								(TmpConsolida.TValors * _CotaPad) / crSigChLcq.Cotas, .t., ;
								_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
								_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, TmpConsolida.TMoedas, _CotaPad, ;
								TmpConsolida.TValors, Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPend2s='S', 0, ;
								TmpConsolida.TValors ), pcidc)

					=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

					pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
					Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
							docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
							sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves ) ;
						Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
								_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, TmpConsolida.TMoedas, _CotaPad, ;
								TmpConsolida.TValors, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
								_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
								((TmpConsolida.TValors * _CotaPad) / crSigChLcq.Cotas), 0, pcidc)

					=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

					Select TmpConsolida

				EndScan
			Endif
			Zap In TmpConsolida
			Store 0 to _TotalMov
		EndIf

		lcCodigo = TmpChq.Umovs
		vVal = 0
		Select crSigCdOpt
		Seek TmpChq.Umovs
		vNLote = fGerUniqueKey('LOTECH')
		Select crSigChLcq
		Scatter To Tmp 
		Append Blank
		Gather From Tmp
		_GrpDep = crSigCdOpt.Grudeps 
		_CntDep = crSigCdOpt.ConDeps 
		Replace Codigos With TmpChq.Umovs, NumLotes with vNLote, ;
				ValTots With vVal,         ValConvs With (vVal / crSigChLcq.Cotas),;
				Grupos  With _GrpDep,      Contas With _CntDep
	EndIf

	vVal = vVal + TmpChq.Valors
	lcOldNumos   = ''
	lcOldCodigos = ''
	lcOldGrupos  = ''
	lcOldContas  = ''
	lcOldEmps    = ''
	
	Select crSigCdOpt
	Seek TmpChq.Umovs
	
	LaCampo5(1,1)='Bancos'
	LaCampo5(2,1)='Agencias'
	LaCampo5(3,1)='Ncontas'
	LaCampo5(4,1)='Ncheques'
	LaCampo5(1,2)=TmpChq.Bancos
	LaCampo5(2,2)=TmpChq.Agencias
	LaCampo5(3,2)=TmpChq.ncontas
	LaCampo5(4,2)=TmpChq.ncheques
	
	If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@laCampo5)
		Select Crcheque
		Go Top
		lcUltGruDests = CrCheque.Grupos
		lcUltConDests = CrCheque.Contas
		_GrupoAnt     = CrCheque.Grupos
		_ContaAnt     = CrCheque.Contas
		_ValorAnt     = CrCheque.Valors
		_CotaAnt      = fCarregarCambio(crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, crSigChLcq.DtEnts)
		_MoedaAnt     = crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs
		
		Num = CrCheque.Nums
		
		If Thisform.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCqChm','CrChm',@LaCampo5)
			If !Empty(CrChm.Numos)
				lcOldEmps    = CrChm.Emps 
				lcOldNumos   = CrChm.Numos
				lcOldCodigos = CrChm.Codigos
			endif
		Endif
		
		Select CrSigCqChm
		Append blank
		Replace DtEnts     With crSigChLcq.DtEnts  ;
				DtSis 	   With _pData;
				codigos	   With crSigChLcq.codigos ;
				nums       With num     ;
				tipos      With crSigCdOpt.tipos   ;
				usuarios   With usuar ;
				numlotes   With crSigChLcq.numlotes;
				autos      With .f.   ;
				datatrans  With crSigChLcq.datatrans ;
				Emps	   With _Empr ;
				contas     With crSigChLcq.Contas  ;
				grupos     With crSigChLcq.Grupos  ;
				iclis	   With TmpChq.iclis   ;
				grclis     With TmpChq.grclis  ;
				moeds	   With crSigChLcq.moeds   ;
				Cotas      With crSigChLcq.Cotas   ;
				ValTots    With vVal ;
				ValConvs   With (vVal / crSigChLcq.Cotas) ;
				bancos 	   With TmpChq.bancos  ;
				agencias   With TmpChq.agencias;
				ncontas	   With TmpChq.ncontas ;
				ncheques   With TmpChq.ncheques;
				NAlineas   With TmpChq.NAlineas;
				Protocolos With TmpChq.Protocolos;
				GruCarts   With CrCheque.GruCarts ;
				ConCarts   With CrCheque.ConCarts ;
				CidChaves  With fUniqueIds()
	
		If Thisform.pcEscolha='INSERIR' And crSigCdOpt.EmiProts = 'S'
			lcProtocolos = Trans(fGerUniqueKey('PROTOCOLOCHQ'),"@L 99999999")
		Else	
			lcProtocolos = ''
		Endif
		Replace Protocolos With lcProtocolos

		lcUpDate = [UpDate SigChe Set Umovs = ']+TmpChq.Umovs+[' , Contas=']+CrSigChLcq.Contas+[' ]+;
				   [, Grupos=']+CrSigChLcq.grupos[' ,Protocolos=']+lcProtocolos+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums=']+CrCheque.Nums+[']

		LnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		_NOper = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
		_VOper = _Empr + Alltrim( Str( _NOper, 9 ) ) 
		_NOper2 = _NOper

		_Dep = .f.
		If Seek( crSigChe.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'Fpags' ) And crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1 And crSigCdOpt.BxPends = 'S'

				Select CrPrBx
				Scan For Valos # 0
					If !Deposits Or ( crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'DB' And crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S' )
						_GrupoOrig = crPrbx.Grupos

						_GrupoDest = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
						_ContaDest = crSigCdPam.contarecs
						If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
							_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
									iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;
							        ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
						Else
							_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
							If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
								_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
							Endif
						Endif

						_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')

						_Valor = crPrbx.Valos
						If _Valor < 0
							_saveoper = _opers
							_opers = _sopers
							_sopers = _saveoper
							_valor = Abs( _valor )
						Endif
						If Round( _Valor, 2 ) # 0
							pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
							Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
									docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
									sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, shists, CidChaves ) ;
								Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TmpChq.Vencs, ;
										_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crPrBx.Moedas, _Cot, ;
										_Valor, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
										crPrBx.Moedas, _Cot, _Valor, 0, 'X', pcidc)

							pCidC    = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.sigkey 

							Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
									docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
									sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, shists, CidChaves ) ;
								Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TmpChq.Vencs, ;
										_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, crPrBx.Moedas, _Cot, ;
										_Valor, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
										crPrBx.Moedas, _Cot, _Valor, _ValPags, 'X', pcidc)

							=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

							_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')

						EndIf
						
						lldep = .t.
						
						lcUpdate = [Update SigCdPbx Set Deposits = ?llDep Where CidChaves = ']+CrPrBx.Cidchaves+[']
						lnErro   = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')

							MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
							Return .f.
						EndIf

					Endif

					lcWher   = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Emps=']+CrPrBx.Emps+[' And Dopes=']+CrPrBx.Dopes+[']+;
							   [ And Numes=]+Str(CrPrBx.Numes,6)

						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf
					
					Select CrPar
					Scan
						If _Cheque = bancos + agencias + contas + numeros
							lcpago = '0'
							ldDat = _pData
							
							lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
										  [Set VPags = ] + Str(CrPar.Valos,12,2) + [, ] + ;
										  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
										  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
									    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']

							lnErro   = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
							IF lnErro < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf
						Endif
					Endscan
					_Dep = .T.
				Endscan
			Else

				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+Tmpchq.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+TmpChq.Agencias+[']+;
						 [ And contas=']+TmpChq.NContas+[' And Numeros=']+Tmpchq.Ncheques+[']

				LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				Select CrPar
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0
					lcpago = '0'
					ldDat = _pData
					
					lcUpdate = [Update SigMvPar ] + ;
								  [Set VPags = Valos, ] + ;
								  	  [Pagos = ?lcPago, ] + ;
								  	  [DtAlts = ?ldDat ] + ;
							    [Where Cidchaves = '] + CrPar.CidChaves + [']

					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf

					If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )

								iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;					
								' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
					Else

						If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
							_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
						Endif
					Endif

					_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')

						_saveoper = _opers
						_opers = _sopers
						_sopers = _saveoper
						_valor = Abs( _valor )
					Endif
					If Round( _Valor, 2 ) # 0
						pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
						Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
								docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
								sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
							Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TmpChq.Vencs, ;
									_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;

					=Seek( crPar.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' )

			Endif
		Else

			Select crSigCdOpt
			=Seek( CrChm.Codigos, 'crSigCdOpt', 'Operacao' )

			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Vopers=?lcNumo ]
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')
			
			Select CrMccr
			Scan
				If (Grupos + Contas = crCheque.GruOrigs + crCheque.ConOrigs And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] And crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S') Or ;
					(Grupos + Contas = crCheque.Grupos + crCheque.Contas And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] And crSigCdOpt.ValPend2s = 'S' )
					lcpago = '0'

					ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = Valors, pagos = ?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
				Endif

			=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
		Endif
***********************************************************************************************		
		If Not Empty( crCheque.FPags ) And Seek( crCheque.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'FPags' ) And ;
				crSigCdOpt.BxPends = 'S' And crSigOpFp.BxComChqs = 1
			Create Cursor TmpComiss ( Emps c(3), Dopes c(20), Numes n(6), Valos n(12,2), Moedas c(3), Nopers n(6), Cotacaos n(12,4))
			Index on Emps+dopes+Str(Numes,6)+moedas+str(nopers,9) Tag Empdopnum

				Return .f.

			laCampo3(1,1)='Nopers'
			laCampo3(1,2)=CrPrbx.Nopers

			Go top

									Values ( CrPar.Emps, CrPar.Dopes, CrPar.Numes, CrPrbx.Moedas, CrPrbx.Nopers, CrPrbx.Cotacaos )

					laCampo3(2,1)='Emps'

							_ValOpera = crEest.Valos
						Else

							_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
						Endif
					Endif

						Return .f.

					Scan
						_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )

						If _Baixar # 0

							lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrSigCdCmm.Baixas,12,2)+;
									   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrSigCdCmm.CidChaves+[']
							LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')

								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf

							If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'
								xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
								xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
								_Hist = crEest.Emps + Alltrim( crEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( crEest.Mascnum ) + ' - Comissão'
							Else
								xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
								xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
								_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
								        iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;
										' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'
							Endif
							If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
								If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'
									_Hist = crEest.Emps + Alltrim( crEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( crEest.Mascnum ) + ' - Comissão'
								Else
									_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
											iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;
											' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'

								lnCota = fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnMoeda = TmpGccr.ComMoedas
							Else
								Go Top in crSigCdPam
								If crSigCdPam.ComMoedas # crSigCdCmm.Moedas And !Empty( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas )
									lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
									lnCota = fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
									lnMoeda = crSigCdPam.ComMoedas
								Else
									lnValor = _Baixar
									lnCota = 1
									lnMoeda = crSigCdCmm.Moedas
								EndIf
							Endif

							pCidC = Dtos(ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao))+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao))),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
							Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
									opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
									sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, shists, CidChaves ) ;
								Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, _pData, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
										_hist, xoper, xsoper, lnMoeda, lnCota, lnValor, .t., ;
										SigCdCmm.Grupos, SigCdCmm.Contas, SigCdPam.ComGrupos, SigCdPam.ComContas, ;
										lnMoeda, lnCota, lnValor, 'X', pcidc)

							=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

							ThisForm.FazContra
						Endif
					Endscan
				Endscan
			Else

				lcWher = [Select * From SigMvPar Where Bancos=']+TmpChq.Bancos+[' And Agencias=']+TmpChq.Agencias+[']+;
						 [ And contas=']+TmpChq.NContas+[' And Numeros=']+TmpChq.Ncheques+[']

				LnErro = ThisForm.PoDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrPar')
				IF lnErro < 1
					MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
					Return .f.
				EndIf

				Select CrPar
				Go Top
				If Reccount() > 0
					_ValBaixa = crPar.Valos
					_ChaveEest = crPar.Emps + crPar.Dopes + Str( crPar.Numes, 6 )

					=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigMvCab','CrEest','EmpDopNums',_ChaveEest)
					=ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdOpe','CrOpe','Dopes',CrPar.dopes)

						Else
							_ValOpera = crEest.Valos / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
							_ValBaixa = _ValBaixa / fCarregarCambio( crOpe.CMoes, crEest.Datas )
						Endif
					Endif
					_PercBaixa = _ValBaixa / _ValOpera

					lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCmm Where Emps=']+CrPar.emps+[' And Dopes=']+crPar.Dopes+[' ]+;
							 [And Numes=]+Str(crpar.Numes,6)
					
					LnErro = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCmm')
					IF lnErro < 1
						MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
						Return .f.
					EndIf

					Select CrSigCdCmm
					Scan
						_Baixar = Round( Comissaos * _PercBaixa, 2 )
						Replace crSigCdCmm.Baixas With crSigCdCmm.Baixas + ;
													 IIf(crSigCdOpt.Tipos='CR',1,-1) * _Baixar
						If _Baixar # 0
							lcUpDate = [UpDate SigCdCmm Set Baixas = ]+Str(CrSigCdCmm.Baixas,12,2)+;
									   [ Where CidChaves = ']+CrSigCdCmm.CidChaves+[']
							LnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
							IF lnErro < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
								Return .f.
							EndIf

							If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'						
								xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
								xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
							Else
								xoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'D', 'C' )
								xsoper = iif( crope.copers=1, 'C', 'D' )
							Endif
							If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
								If crSigCdOpt.Tipos = 'CR'						
									_Hist = crEest.Emps + Alltrim( crEest.Dopes ) + ' ' + Alltrim( crEest.Mascnum ) + ' - Comissão'
								Else
									_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
											iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;									
											' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) ) + ' - Est. Comissão'
								Endif
							Else
								_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
								If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
									_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
								Endif
							Endif
							lcSql = [Select ComMoedas From SigCdGcr Where Codigos = ']+CrSigCdCmm.Grupos+[']
							If Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpGccr') < 1
								MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão Inserir(TmpGccr)')
								Return .f.
							EndIf
							If Not Empty(TmpGccr.ComMoedas) And TmpGccr.ComMoedas # CrSigCdCmm.Moedas
								lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnCota = fCarregarCambio( TmpGccr.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
								lnMoeda = TmpGccr.ComMoedas
							Else
								Go Top in crSigCdPam
								If crSigCdPam.ComMoedas # crSigCdCmm.Moedas And !Empty( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas )
									lnValor = _Baixar * fCarregarCambio( crSigCdCmm.Moedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) ) / fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
									lnCota = fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.ComMoedas, ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao) )
									lnMoeda = crSigCdPam.ComMoedas
								Else
									lnValor = _Baixar
									lnCota = 1
									lnMoeda = crSigCdCmm.Moedas
								EndIf
							Endif
							pCidC = Dtos(ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao))+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(ttod(ThisForm.DataPadrao))),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
							Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
									opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
									sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, shists, CidChaves ) ;
								Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, _pData, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
										_hist, xoper, xsoper, lnMoeda, lnCota, lnValor, .t., ;
										crSigCdCmm.Grupos, crSigCdCmm.Contas, crSigCdPam.ComGrupos, crSigCdPam.ComContas, ;
										lnMoeda, lnCota, lnValor, 'X', pcidc)

							=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
							ThisForm.FazContra
							_Noper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
						Endif
					Endscan
				EndIf
			EndIf
		EndIf
***********************************************************************************************		
		If ( Seek( crCheque.FPags, 'crSigOpFp', 'Fpags' ) And crSigOpFp.ValPends = 1 And crSigCdOpt.BxPends # 'S' ) Or ( crSigCdOpt.FazContas = 'S' And Empty( crCheque.FPags ) )
			_GrupoOrig = crSigCdPam.gruporecs
			_ContaOrig = crSigCdPam.contarecs
			_GrupoDest = crCheque.GruOrigs
			_ContaDest = crCheque.ConOrigs

			If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
						iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;			
						' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
			Else
				_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
					_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
				Endif
			Endif
			_Opers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','D','C') 
			_Sopers = iif(crSigCdOpt.tipos='CR','C','D')
			If Round( TmpChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, 2 ) # 0
				pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
				Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
						docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
						sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves ) ;
					Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TmpChq.Vencs, ;
							_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
							TmpChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, .t., _GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
							_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, TmpChq.valors, 0, pcidc)

				=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
						
				pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.Sigkey 
				Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
						docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
						sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves ) ;
					Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, TmpChq.Vencs, ;
							_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs, 1, ;
							TmpChq.valors, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
							_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, TmpChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
							Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPends = 'S' , 0, TmpChq.valors/crSigChLcq.Cotas ), pcidc )

				=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')

			Endif

			_GrupoAnt = _GrupoOrig
			_ContaAnt = _ContaOrig
			_ValorAnt = TmpChq.Valors / crSigChLcq.Cotas
			_CotaAnt  = crSigChLcq.Cotas
			_MoedaAnt = crSigChLcq.Moeds

		Endif
	
		_TotalMov = _TotalMov + TmpChq.Valors
				
		Select crSigCqChm
		Replace numos with _NOper

		lcUpDate = [UpDate SigChe Set Numos = ]+Str(_Noper)+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums=']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
		LnErro   = ThisForm.PoDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
		IF lnErro < 1
			MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
			Return .f.
		EndIf

		If !Empty(crSigCdOpt.Ccentrals)
			Declare laCampo4(3,2)
			laCampo4(1,1)='Grupos'
			laCampo4(1,2)=Crcheque.GrClis
			laCampo4(2,1)='Contas'
			laCampo4(2,2)=CrCheque.Iclis
			laCampo4(1,1)='Cocos'
			laCampo4(1,2)=CrSigCdOpt.CCentrals
			If !ThisForm.podatamgr.ChkRegister('SigClLan',@laCampo4)
				Select CrSigClLan
				Append Blank
				Replace Grupos With Crcheque.GrClis,;
						Contas With Crcheque.Iclis,;
						Cocos  With crSigCdOpt.Ccentrals,;
						CidChaves With fUniqueIds(),;
						GruContas With Grupos + Contas
			Endif
		Endif
		If crSigCdOpt.HistClis = 'S'	&& Lança Histórico do cliente
			If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				lcHistClis = crSigChLcq.Emps + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ;
							 iif(Empty(TmpChq.NAlineas),'',' ' + AllTrim(Str(TmpChq.NAlineas))) + ;
							 ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
			Else
				lcHistClis = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
					lcHistClis = lcHistClis + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
				Endif
			Endif
			lcHistClis = lcHistClis + ' (' + TmpChq.Bancos + '-' + TmpChq.Agencias + '-' + ;
	                     TmpChq.Ncontas+ '-' + TmpChq.NCheques + ' ' + ;
	                     AllTrim(Transf(TmpChq.Valors,'999,999,999.99')) + ')'
		
			If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCdCli','CrtmpCli','Iclis',CrCheque.Iclis)
				Select CrtmpCli
				Go Top
				lcGruCliH = Evaluate('crtmpCli.Grupos')
			Else
				lcGruCliH = crSigChe.GrClis
			EndIf			

			If !Empty(lcHistClis)
				Insert Into crSigCdClh (Autos, Grupos, Iclis, Datas, Hists, Emps, Nopers, CidChaves, GruContas ) Values ;
									(.T., lcGruCliH, crCheque.Iclis, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, ;
									lcHistClis, crSigCqChm.Emps, crSigCqChm.Numos, fUniqueIds(), lcGruCliH + CrCheque.Iclis) 
			EndIf
		EndIf

		_GrupoDest = crSigChLcq.Grupos
		_ContaDest = crSigChLcq.Contas
		_ValorsAnt = TmpChq.Valors
		_CotaAnt   = fCarregarCambio( crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs,crSigChLcq.DtEnts )
		_MoedaAnt  = crSigCdPam.MoedaCheqs
		lcProcura  = lcUltGruDests + lcUltConDests + _MoedaAnt

		If !Seek(lcProcura,'TmpConsolida','TGruConMoe')
			Insert Into TmpConsolida (TGrupos, TContas, TMoedas, TValors, TCotas) Values ;
				(lcUltGruDests, lcUltConDests, _MoedaAnt, _ValorsAnt, _CotaAnt)
		Else
			Select TmpConsolida
			Replace TValors With TValors + _ValorsAnt
		EndIf
	EndIf
	
	lcUpDate = [UpDAte SigChe Set Nalineas=]+Str(TmpChq.Nalineas)+[ , Umovs=']+CrSigChLcq.codigos+[' ]+;
			   [, Grupos=']+CrSigChLcq.Grupos+[' , contas=']+CrSigChLcq.contas+[' , DtAlts = ?lDtAlt Where Nums=']+CrCheque.Nums+[']
	lnErro   = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcUpDate,'')
	IF lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return .f.
	EndIf

	If !Empty(lcOldNumos)
		lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where Emps=']+lcOldEmps+[' And Nopers=']+lcOldNumos+[']
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrMccr')

		Select crMccr
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0
			=Seek(lcOldCodigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
			lcOldVOpers = crMccr.VOpers
			lcWher = [Select * From SigMvCcr Where VOpers=']+lcOldVoper+[']
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrvMccr')
			Select CrvMccr
			Scan
				If ( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = crSigChe.GruOrigs + crSigChe.ConOrigs And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] ) Or ;
					( crvMccr.Grupos + crvMccr.Contas = lcOldGrupos + lcOldContas And Left(SHists, 1) <> [X] )
					lcpago = '0'
					lcDtps = CrvMccr.Dopcs + CrvMccr.Opers + CrvMccr.Tipos + lcpago
					ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute([UpDate SigMvCcr Set ValPags = Valors, pagos = ?lcpago, DopoTps = ?lcDtps Where Cidchaves = ']+CrvMccr.CidChaves+['],'')
				Endif
			Endscan
		EndIf
	Endif

	=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
	Select TempChq
EndScan

If !Empty(lcCodigo)
	Select crSigChLcq
	Replace ValTots With vVal
	If _TotalMov # 0
		Select TmpConsolida
		Scan
			_GrupoDest = TmpConsolida.TGrupos
			_ContaDest = TmpConsolida.TContas
			_GrupoOrig = crSigChLcq.Grupos
			_ContaOrig = crSigChLcq.Contas
			_CotaPad   = TmpConsolida.TCotas
			_Opers     = 'D'
			_SOpers    = 'C'

			If Empty( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				_Hist = _empr + '-' + Alltrim( crSigChLcq.codigos ) + ' : ' + alltrim( str( crSigChLcq.numlotes, 8 ) )
			Else
				_Hist = Alltrim( crSigCdOpt.Hists )
				If crSigCdOpt.TpHists = 1
					_Hist = _Hist + ' ' + Alltrim( Str( crSigChLcq.NumLotes ) )
				Endif
			Endif
			
			_NOper2 = fGerUniqueKey('SigMvCcr')
			pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.sigkey 
			Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
					docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
					sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, Cidchaves ) ;
				Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
						_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _Opers, _SOpers, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
						(TmpConsolida.TValors * _CotaPad) / crSigChLcq.Cotas, .t., ;
						_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, ;
						_GrupoDest, _ContaDest, TmpConsolida.TMoedas, _CotaPad, ;
						TmpConsolida.TValors, Iif( crSigCdOpt.ValPend2s='S', 0, ;
						TmpConsolida.TValors ), pcidc )

			=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)

			pCidC = Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)+Transform(fGerUniqueKey(Dtos(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts)),"@L 999999")+ThisForm.sigkey 
			Insert Into crSigMvCcr ( NOpers, VOpers, Emps, Datas, usualts, datalts, Vencs, hists,;
					docus, opers, sopers, moedas, cotacaos, valors, autos, grupos, contas, ;
					sgrupos, scontas, smoedas, scotacaos, svalors, valpags, CidChaves ) ;
				Values ( _NOper2, _VOper, _empr, crSigChLcq.DtEnts, usuar, _pData, _pData, ;
						_hist, str( _NOper, 9 ), _SOpers, _Opers, TmpConsolida.TMoedas, _CotaPad, ;
						TmpConsolida.TValors, .t., _GrupoDest, _ContaDest, ;
						_GrupoOrig, _ContaOrig, crSigChLcq.Moeds, crSigChLcq.Cotas, ;
						(TmpConsolida.TValors * _CotaPad) / crSigChLcq.Cotas, 0, pcidc)

			=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
			Select TmpConsolida
		EndScan
	Endif
EndIf

Select crSigChLcq
Set Order to NumLotes
Seek (lcNumLoteAtual)
RETURN .t.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE novoregistro
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE novoregistro
LParameters pBott

ThisForm.ValCheques()

=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'CrSigCdOpt','Operacao')
Select TempChq
Set Order to
Goto Bottom
If Eof() Or (!Eof() And !Empty(Bancos+Agencias+NContas+NCheques))
	Append Blank
	Replace Emps With crSigChLcq.Emps
	=Seek(crSigChLcq.Emps,'crSigCdEmp','CEmps')
	Replace DEmps  With crSigCdEmp.Razas, ;
			GruRes With CrSigCdOpt.Respons
			
	If Not Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons) And Seek(CrSigCdOpt.Respons,'crSigCdGcr','Codigos')
		Replace dGruRes With crSigCdGcr.Descrs
	EndIf			
	
	If CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And !Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis)
		lcCliente = crSigChLcq.Iclis
		lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
		=Thisform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
		Select CrTmpCli
		Go Top
		Select TempChq
		Replace Grclis With crtmpCli.Grupos, ;
				Iclis  With crSigChLcq.Iclis, ;
				Cpfs   With crtmpCli.Cpfs, ;
				Nomes  With crtmpCli.RClis
		With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntConta
			.Get_Conta.Refresh			
			.Get_CPF.Refresh
			.Get_Nome.Refresh
		EndWith
	EndIf
	
	If CrSigCdOpt.nDtEmiss = 1
		Select TempChq
		Replace TempChq.Datas With crSigChLcq.DtEnts
	EndIf

	If ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.chk_FollowUp.Value And CrSigCdOpt.Obs = [S]
		Replace TempChq.Obs With ThisForm.followUp
	Endif
EndIf

Select TempChq
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.Refresh
Thisform.RefreshCnts()
ThisForm.Leitor = .F.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE refreshcnts
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE refreshcnts
Select TempChq

lcQuery = [Select FPags ] + ;
		    [From SigMvPar ] + ;
		   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + TempChq.EmpDopNums + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Bancos = '] + TempChq.Bancos + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Agencias = '] + TempChq.Agencias + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Contas = '] + TempChq.NContas + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [Numeros = '] + TempChq.NCheques + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, [LocalPar]) < 1)
	=MessageBox([Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!], 16, [Falha na Conexão (LocalPar)])
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In LocalPar
If Not Eof([LocalPar])
	Thisform.Pagina.Dados.getCondPagto.Value = LocalPar.FPags
Else
	Thisform.Pagina.Dados.getCondPagto.Value = []
EndIf

With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque
	If .Visible
		.Get_Cheque.Refresh
		.Get_Bco.Refresh
		.Get_Age.Refresh
		.Get_Conta.Refresh
		.Get_Num.Refresh
		.fweditdata_Venc.Refresh
		.Get_Valor.Refresh
		.Get_Alinea.Refresh
		.Data_Emiss.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith

*** mena - 26/09/2017 - libera campo alinea para digitacao ***
With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque
	If !.Visible and .Get_Alinea.visible
		.visible = .t.
		.Get_Cheque.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Bco.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Age.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Conta.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Num.Enabled = .f.
		.fweditdata_Venc.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Valor.Enabled = .f.
		.Get_Alinea.visible = .T.
		.Data_Emiss.Enabled = .f.
	EndIf
EndWith

With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmpresa
	If .Visible
		.Get_Emps.Refresh
		.Get_dEmps.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith

With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntConta
	If .Visible
		.Get_Conta.Refresh
		.Get_Cpf.Refresh
		.Get_Nome.Refresh		
	EndIf
EndWith

With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel
	If .Visible
		.Get_GruRes.Refresh
		.Get_ConRes.Refresh		
		.Get_dConRes.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith
With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntfollowUp
	If .Visible
		.Get_Obs.Refresh
	EndIf
EndWith

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE sqldados
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE sqldados
llDatai = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_final.Value
llDatai = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Max(llDatai,CtoD('01/01/1900')))+'000000'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])
llDataf = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Max(llDataf,CtoD('01/01/1900')))+'235959'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])
With ThisForm.Pagina.Lista
	If .Cmb_Concilia.Value = 1
		llCmbt = .t.
		llCmbf = .f.
	Else
		If .Cmb_Concilia.Value = 2
			llCmbt = .t.
			llCmbf = .t.
		Else
			llCmbt = .f.
			llcmbf = .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndWith

ThisForm.podatamgr.Requery('crSigChLcq')
Select crSigChLcq
Index On NumLotes Tag Numlotes
Index On Numlotes Tag Numlotesc For Concs
Index On NumLotes Tag Numlotesn For !Concs
Go Top

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE valcheques
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE valcheques
Local lnRecno, lnValTot, lnNumTot, lcAlias

lcAlias = Alias()

Select [ ] as Agrupar, Sum(Valors) As TotValors, Count(1) As TotCheques ;
	From TempChq Where Not Empty(Bancos+Agencias+nContas+nCheques) Into Cursor csTotal group by 1

Select crSigChLcq
Replace ValTots With csTotal.TotValors ;
		ValConvs With Round( csTotal.TotValors / iif(crSigChLcq.Cotas # 0,crSigChLcq.Cotas,1), 2 )

With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntInfos
	.Get_Qtde.Value = csTotal.TotCheques
	.Get_Valor.Refresh
	.Get_ValConv.Refresh
Endwith

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.get_ValorTotal.Refresh
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Get_QtdeChqs.Value = csTotal.TotCheques

Select (lcAlias)

Return (csTotal.TotCheques)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Init
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Init
LPARAMETERS _pConsulta, _pEmps, _pNopers

Private llCmbt, llCmbf, llDatai, llDataf

ThisForm.podatamgr = CreateObject('fSqlConector','SigChe')

pData = ThisForm.PodataMgr.GetDatetime()
ThisForm.DataPadrao = pData

If Ttod(ThisForm.Datapadrao) <> Date()
	=MessageBox('A Data do Micro não confere com Data do Servidor.'+Chr(13)+;
				'Registrando ocorrencia Usuário: '+Usuar,0,'',5000)
Endif

ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.Value = Ttod(pData)
ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_Final.Value = Ttod(pData)
ThisForm.LocRegister = 0

llDatai = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_final.Value
llDatai = fDtoSQL(llDatai)
llDataf = fDtoSQL(llDataf,'23:59:59')
llCmbt  = .F.
llCmbf  = .T.
lnOper  = 9999999
lcEmpo  = '   '
lcVope  = 99999999
lnLote  = 99999999
lnNum   = '99999999'
lcCliente= ' '
Cidchav = Space(20)

lcQuerylchm  = [Select * From SigChLcq where dtents between ?llDatai And ?llDataf And (Concs=?llCmbf Or Concs=?llCmbt) ]
lcQueryCot   = [Select * From SigCdCot ]
lcQueRyMoe   = [Select * From SigCdMoe ] 
lcQueryMccr  = [Select * From SigMvCcr where Nopers = ?lNoper ]
lcQueryChm   = [Select b.* From SigCqChm b, SigChe a Where b.NumLotes = ?lnLote And b.Numos = a.Numos And b.Nums = a.Nums ]  &&[Select * From SigCqChm where NumLotes = ?lnLote ]
lcQueryOper  = [Select * From SigCdOpt Where Not Inativas = 1]
lcQueryGccr  = [Select * From SigCdGcr ]
lcQueryCli   = [Select * From SigCdCli Where Iclis = ?lcCliente ]
lcQueryUsu   = [Select * From SigCdUsu Where Not cAtivos='N']
lcQueryCarg  = [Select * From SigCdCrg  ]
lcQueryFpag  = [Select * From SigOpFp ]
lcQueryEmp   = [Select * From SigCdEmp ]
lcQueryParam = [Select gesind, moedetqs, grupoests, contaests, transfres, empmasters, SaldConcs, ]+;
			   [GrupoRecs, grupopags, contarecs, contapags, MoedaCheqs, GruCarts, ConCarts, ]+;
			   [GruCheques, ConCheques, GrpadClis, TpInstalas, ComMoedas, ComGrupos, ComContas ]+;
			   [From SigCdPam ]
lcQueryferia = [Select * From SigCdFer ]
lcQueryChe   = [Select * From SigChe Where Nums = ?LnNum ]
lcQueryPrbx  = [Select * From SigCdPbx Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQueryPar   = [Select * From SigMvPar  Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQueryClih  = [Select * From SigCdClh Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQueryLanc  = [Select * From SigClLan Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQuerycar   = [Select * From SigCdCar  Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQuerycart  = [Select * From SigCdCtt Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQuerycard  = [Select * From SigCdCrd Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]
lcQueryAlin  = [Select * From SigCdAli ]
lcQueryCheh  = [Select * From SigCheh Where CidChaves = ?Cidchav ]

With ThisForm
	With .podatamgr
		.AddCursor('SigChLcq','NumLotes','crSigChLcq','',ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Grade,lcQuerylchm )
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Numlotes','', '', 'Lote',			 61, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Codigos', '', '', 'Movimentação',	150, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('DtEnts',  '', '', 'Entrada',		 78, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('GruOrigs','', '', 'Grupo/Ori',	 85, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('ConOrigs','', '', 'Conta/Ori',	 85, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Grupos',  '', '', 'Grupo/Des',	 85, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Contas',  '', '', 'Conta/Des',	 85, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Valtots', '', '', 'Valor',		105, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Moeds',   '', '', 'Moeda',		 40, .t.)
			.pfSqlTabela(1).pColuna('Usuarios','', '', 'Usuário',		 80, .t.)
			
		.AddCursor('SigCdCli','Iclis','CrSigCdCli','','')
		.AddCursor('SigCdCot'  ,'cidchaves' ,'CrSigCdCot'  ,'','',lcQueryCot)
		.AddCursor('SigCdMoe'  ,'cmoes'     ,'CrSigCdMoe'  ,'','',lcQueryMoe)
		.AddCursor('SigMvCcr' ,'Cidchaves' ,'CrSigMvCcr' ,'','',lcQueryMccr)
		.AddCursor('SigCdOpt' ,'Operacaos' ,'crSigCdOpt' ,'','',lcQueryOper)
		.AddCursor('SigCqChm'  ,'CidChaves' ,'CrSigCqChm'  ,'','',lcQueryChm)
		.AddCursor('SigCdEmp'  ,'cemps'     ,'CrSigCdEmp'  ,'','',lcQueryEmp)
		.AddCursor('SigCdGcr','Codigos','CrSigCdGcr','','',lcQueryGccr)
		.AddCursor('SigCdUsu','Usuarios','CrSigCdUsu','','',lcQueryUsu)
		.Addcursor('SigCdCrg','cCargs','CrSigCdCrg','','',lcQueryCarg)
		.Addcursor('SigOpFp','fpags','CrSigOpFp','','',lcQueryfpag)
		.AddCursor('SigCdPam','empmasters','CrSigCdPam','','',lcQueryParam)
		.Addcursor('SigCdFer','datas','CrSigCdFer','','',lcQueryferia)
		.Addcursor('SigChe','Nums','CrSigChe','','',lcQueryChe)
		.Addcursor('SigCdPbx','Cidchaves','CrSigCdPbx','','',lcQueryPrbx)
		.Addcursor('SigMvPar','Cidchaves','CrSigMvPar','','',lcQueryPar)
		.Addcursor('SigCdAli','Codigos','CrSigCdAli','','',lcQueryAlin)
		.Addcursor('SigCdClh','Cidchaves','CrSigCdClh','','',lcQueryClih)
		.Addcursor('SigClLan','Cidchaves','CrSigClLan','','',lcQueryLanc)
		.Addcursor('SigCdCar','Cidchaves','CrSigCdCar','','',lcQuerycar)
		.Addcursor('SigCdCtt','Cidchaves','CrSigCdCtt','','',lcQuerycart)
		.Addcursor('SigCdCrd','Cidchaves','CrSigCdCrd','','',lcQuerycard)
		.Addcursor('SigCheh','Cidchaves','CrSigCheh','','',lcQuerycheh)
	EndWith
	= DoDefault()
	.LockScreen = .F.
EndWith

ThisForm.poDataMgr.CursorQuery('SigCdPac', 'crSigCdPac')
ThisForm.SigKey = CrSigCdPac.SigKeys

Thisform.podatamgr.Requery('CrSigCdCot')
ThisForm.podatamgr.Requery('CrSigCdMoe')
ThisForm.podatamgr.Requery('crSigCdOpt')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdEmp')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdGcr')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdUsu')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdCrg')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigOpFp')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdPam')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdFer')
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdAli')

Select CrSigCdCot
Index On Cmoes+Dtos(Datas)+horas Tag cmoedata
Index On cmoes+DTOS(datas)+horas Tag Cotacaos

Select CrSigCdMoe
Index on Dmoes Tag Dmoes
Index on Cmoes tag Cmoes

Select CrSigCdUsu
Index on Usuarios Tag Usuarios

Select CrSigCdCrg
Index on cCargs Tag Ccargs

Select CrSigCdGcr
Index on Codigos Tag Codigos

Select CrSigOpFp
Index on Fpags Tag Fpags

Select crSigCdOpt
Index On Operacaos Tag Operacao
Index On Descrs    Tag Descrs

Select CrSigCdEmp
Index On Cemps Tag Cemps

Select CrSigCdFer
Index On Dtos(datas) Tag Datas

Select CrSigCdAli
Index On Descrs  Tag Descrs
Index On Codigos Tag Codigos

ThisForm.SqlDados
ThisForm.CriaCursor

ThisForm.TamGrpBotoes = ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cmdGrpBotoes.Width

ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.LimpaDT.Visible = fChecaAcesso([SigChe],[LIMPADT]) 
ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Conciliação.Visible = fChecaAcesso( 'SigChe', 'CONCILIA' )
ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Grade.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','Iif(crSigChLcq.Concs=.T.,RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0))','Column')
ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Grade.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(crSigChLcq.Autos=.T.,RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')

lcSql = [Select TitOpcS, TitOpcN, TitOpcA From SigCdPac]
If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'LocalParac') < 1
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalParac)')
	Return .f.
EndIf
Select LocalParac
Go Top
With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.OptSnA
	.option1.Caption = Iif(Not Empty(LocalParac.TitOpcs),LocalParac.TitOpcS,[Sim])
	.option2.Caption = Iif(Not Empty(LocalParac.TitOpcn),LocalParac.TitOpcN,[Nao])
	.option3.Caption = Iif(Not Empty(LocalParac.TitOpcA),LocalParac.TitOpcA,[Ambos])
EndWith


With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque 
	.RecordSource             = 'TempChq'
	.Banco.ControlSource      = 'TempChq.Bancos'
	.Agencia.ControlSource    = 'TempChq.Agencias'
	.Conta.ControlSource      = 'TempChq.NContas'
	.Cheque.ControlSource     = 'TempChq.NCheques'
	.Vencimento.ControlSource = 'TempChq.Vencs'
	.Valor.ControlSource      = 'TempChq.Valors'
	.Alinea.ControlSource     = 'TempChq.NAlineas'
	.Empresa.ControlSource    = 'TempChq.Emps'
	.Protocolo.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.Protocolos'
	.DtEmiss.ControlSource    = 'TempChq.Datas'
	.Marca.ControlSource	  = 'TempChq.Marca'
	If CrSigCdOpt.TpEmiss = 2
		.CodIclis.ControlSource     = 'TempChq.Cpfs'
		.CodIclis.Width             = 104
		.CodIclis.Header1.Caption   = 'CPF / CNPJ'
		.CodIclis.Header1.Alignment = 2
		.CodIclis.Header1.FontSize  = 8
	Else
		.CodIclis.ControlSource     = 'TempChq.IClis'
		.CodIclis.Width             = 74
		.CodIclis.Header1.Caption   = 'Código Cliente'
		.CodIclis.Header1.Alignment = 2
		.CodIclis.Header1.FontSize  = 8
	EndIf
	.ReadOnly = .F.
	.SetAll('DynamicForeColor','iif(TempChq.Vencs>crSigChLcq.DtEnts And ThisForm.Aviso,RGB(255,0,0),Iif(Not Empty(TempChq.Obs),RGB(0,0,255),RGB(0,0,0)))','Column')
	.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(Not Empty(TempChq.Obs),RGB(220,255,220),RGB(255,255,255))','Column')
	.SetAll('ReadOnly',.T.,'Column')
	.SetAll('ReadOnly',.T.,'Text1')
	.SetAll('ReadOnly',.F.,'Check1')
	.SetAll('Resizable',.F.,'Column')
	.SetAll('Movable',.F.,'Column')
	
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque
	.Get_Valor.ControlSource    = 'TempChq.Valors'
	.fweditdata_Venc.ControlSource = 'TempChq.Vencs'
	.Get_Alinea.ControlSource   = 'TempChq.NAlineas'
	.Data_Emiss.ControlSource   = 'TempChq.Datas'
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntEmpresa
	.Get_Emps.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.Emps'
	.Get_dEmps.ControlSource = 'TempChq.dEmps'
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta
	.Get_Conta.ControlSource = 'TempChq.Iclis'
	.Get_Cpf.ControlSource   = 'TempChq.Cpfs'
	.Get_Nome.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.Nomes'
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel
	.Get_GruRes.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.GruRes'
	.Get_ConRes.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.ConRes'
	.Get_dConRes.ControlSource = 'TempChq.dConRes'
EndWith

With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntfollowUp
	.Get_Obs.ControlSource  = 'TempChq.Obs'
EndWith

ThisForm.ConsultaExterna = _pConsulta

If _pConsulta
	lcEmpo = _pEmps
	lcVope = _pNopers
	lcWher = [Select * From SigCqChm Where Emps = ']+lcEmpo+[' And Numos = ]+Str(lcVope)
	ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCqChm')
	Select CrSigCqChm
	Go Top
	lnLote = CrSigCqChm.NumLotes
	lcWher = [Select * From SigChLcq Where NumLotes = ]+Str(lnLote)
	ThisForm.poDatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'crSigChLcq')
	Select crSigChLcq
	Go Top
	ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Grupo_op.Click( 2 )
Else
	ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.SetFocus
EndIf


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Load
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Load
=fConfigGeral()

Create Cursor TempChq (Bancos C(3), Agencias C(4), NContas C(10), NCheques C(6), Vencs D(8) null, ;
					   Valors N(12,2), Emps C(3), Iclis C(10), Grclis C(10), contas C(10), grupos C(10), ;
					   valpags n(12,2), GruCarts C(10), ConCarts C(10), NAlineas N(3), Protocolos C(8), ;
					   Cpfs c(20), Nomes c(50), GruRes c(10), ConRes c(10), dGruRes C(30), dConRes C(50), ;
					   dEmps C(50), Leituras C(34), Numeros C(6), Datas D(8) null, Obss m(4) null, ;
					   Obs c(76) Null, Cmc7 c(36), Nums c(8), TemObs n(1), EmpDopNums c(29), marca n(1,0))
Index On Vencs Tag Vencs
Index On Bancos + Agencias + NContas + NCheques Tag Cheque

Index on Bancos Tag Bancos additive 
Index on Agencias Tag Agencias additive
Index on NContas Tag NContas additive
Index on NCheques Tag NCheques additive
Index on Valors Tag Valors additive
Index on NAlineas Tag NAlineas additive
Index on Emps Tag Emps additive
Index on Protocolos Tag Protocolos additive
Index on Datas Tag Datas additive

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE mativapagina1
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE mativapagina1
Private llDatai, llDataf, llCmbt, llCmbf

llDatai = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.cntFiltros.cnt_periodo.Dt_final.Value
llDatai = fDtoSQL(llDatai)
llDataf = fDtoSQL(llDataf,'23:59:59')
With ThisForm.Pagina.Lista
	If .Cmb_Concilia.Value = 1
		llCmbt = .t.
		llCmbf = .f.
	Else
		If .Cmb_Concilia.Value = 2
			llCmbt = .t.
			llCmbf = .t.
		Else
			llCmbt = .f.
			llcmbf = .f.
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndWith

DoDefault()

Select crSigChLcq
Index On NumLotes Tag Numlotes
Set Near On
If ThisForm.Locregister # 0
	Seek ThisForm.Locregister
EndIf
Set Near Off
ThisForm.Pagina.Lista.Grade.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE msv_alterar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE msv_alterar
Local llOk
llOk = ThisForm.Deletar()
If llOk
	llOk = ThisForm.Inserir()
EndIf
If llOk
	llOk = ThisForm.calculasaldo()
EndIf
If llOk
	llOk = Thisform.Podatamgr.Commit() > 0
	contad = thisform.pagina.dados.cntDestino.get_Conta.Value
	contao = thisform.pagina.dados.cntOrigem.get_ConOrig.Value
	ncont = thisform.pagina.dados.cntInfos.get_Qtde.Value
	=fGravarLog('T', [LOTE DE CHEQUE], 'ALTERAR',crSigChLcq.emps + ' ' +  AllTrim(Str(crSigChLcq.NumLotes,6)) + ' ' + TRANSFORM(crSigChLcq.valtots,"@ 9,999,999.99") + ' Qtd:' + STR(ncont) + 'CO:' + contao + 'CD:' + contad,Usuar)
EndIf
If llOk
	=fCriarCurSit(.t.)
Else
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.RollBack()
	MessageBox('Nao foi possivel completar a alteracao. tente novamente!!',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.retorna = .f.
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE msv_excluir
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE msv_excluir
Local llOk
llOk = ThisForm.Deletar()
If llOk
	llOk = ThisForm.calculasaldo()
EndIf
If llOk
	llOk = Thisform.Podatamgr.Commit() > 0
	contad = thisform.pagina.dados.cntDestino.get_Conta.Value
	contao = thisform.pagina.dados.cntOrigem.get_ConOrig.Value
	ncont = thisform.pagina.dados.cntInfos.get_Qtde.Value
	=fGravarLog('T', [LOTE DE CHEQUE], 'EXCLUIR',crSigChLcq.emps + ' ' +  AllTrim(Str(crSigChLcq.NumLotes,6)) + ' ' + TRANSFORM(crSigChLcq.valtots,"@ 9,999,999.99") + ' Qtd:' + STR(ncont) + 'CO:' + contao + 'CD:' + contad,Usuar)
EndIf
If llOk
	=fCriarCurSit(.t.)
Else
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.RollBack()
	MessageBox('Nao foi possivel completar a alteracao. tente novamente!!',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.retorna = .f.
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE msv_inserir
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE msv_inserir
Local llOk
Select CrSigChLcq

llOk = ThisForm.Inserir()
If llOk
	llOk = ThisForm.calculasaldo()
EndIf
If llOk
	llOk = Thisform.Podatamgr.Commit() > 0
	contad = thisform.pagina.dados.cntDestino.get_Conta.Value
	contao = thisform.pagina.dados.cntOrigem.get_ConOrig.Value
	ncont = thisform.pagina.dados.cntInfos.get_Qtde.Value
	=fGravarLog('T', [LOTE DE CHEQUE], 'INSERIR',crSigChLcq.emps + ' ' +  AllTrim(Str(crSigChLcq.NumLotes,6)) + ' ' + TRANSFORM(crSigChLcq.valtots,"@ 9,999,999.99") + ' Qtd:' + STR(ncont) + 'CO:' + contao + 'CD:' + contad,Usuar)
EndIf
If llOk
	=fCriarCurSit(.t.)
Else
	ThisForm.Podatamgr.RollBack()
	MessageBox('Nao foi possivel completar a alteracao. tente novamente!!',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	ThisForm.retorna = .f.
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE msv_procurar
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE msv_procurar
NumLote=crSigChLcq.NumLotes
If !Empty(NumLote)
	ThisForm.Locregister = NumLote
	Set Near On
	=Seek( NumLote, "crSigChLcq", "NumLotes" )
	Set Near Off
EndIf

ENDPROC
