================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque
* Classe: container (Base: container)
* Registro: 106
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Local lcCheque, lnRecNo, llNovoRegistro, lnFoco
Declare LaCampo(4,2)

Select TempChq
If !Eof()
	lnRecNo = RecNo()
	lcCheque = TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.NContas + TempChq.NCheques
	LaCampo(1,1)='Bancos'
	LaCampo(2,1)='Agencias'
	LaCampo(3,1)='nContas'
	LaCampo(4,1)='Ncheques'
	LaCampo(1,2)=TempChq.Bancos
	LaCampo(2,2)=TempChq.Agencias
	LaCampo(3,2)=TempChq.NContas
	LaCampo(4,2)=TempChq.NCheques
Else
	lnRecNo = -1
Endif

lnFoco = 0

If !Eof() And !Bof() And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.NContas + TempChq.NCheques)
	Do Case
		Case Not fChkDigitos(TempChq.Bancos)
			MessageBox('Código de Banco Inválido !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 1

		Case Not fChkDigitos(TempChq.Agencias)
			MessageBox('Código de Agencia Inválida !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 2

		Case Not fChkDigitos(TempChq.NContas)
			MessageBox('Código da Conta do Cheque Inválida !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 3

		Case Not fChkDigitos(TempChq.NCheques)
			MessageBox('Número do Cheque Inválido !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 4
			
		Case Not Thisform.fVerCheque( Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques)
			Thisform.Limpa_Che()
			ThisForm.RefreshCnts()
			lnFoco = 1

		Case CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And Empty(TempChq.Valors)
			MessageBox('Valor do Cheque Inválido !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 5
			
		Case CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And Empty(TempChq.Vencs)
			MessageBox('Vencimento do Cheque Inválido !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 6

		Case CrSigCdOpt.Alineas = 'S' And Empty(TempChq.NAlineas)
			MessageBox('Alínea Inválida !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 7
			
		Case ThisForm.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And ;
				ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque',@LaCampo)
			MessageBox('Cheque já Cadastrado !!!',0+48,'')
			lnFoco = 1
			
		OtherWise
			Select TempChq
			Set Order to Cheque
			Seek lcCheque
			If RecNo() # lnRecNo
				MessageBox('Cheque já Utilizado Nessa Movimentação !!!',0+48,'')
				lnFoco = 1
			Else
				Skip
				If !Eof() And TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.NContas + TempChq.NCheques = lcCheque
					MessageBox('Cheque já Utilizado Nessa Movimentação !!!',0+48,'')
					lnFoco = 1
				EndIf
			EndIf
	EndCase
EndIf

ThisForm.ValCheques()

Select TempChq
Set Order to
If lnRecNo # -1
	Goto (lnRecNo)
EndIf

llNovoRegistro = .F.
If !Eof() And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos + TempChq.Agencias + TempChq.NContas + TempChq.NCheques)
	Do Case
		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And ;
					!Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And CrSigCdOpt.EmpInds = 'S'
			llNovoRegistro = .F.

		Case InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
			         CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And ;
		   	 		(!Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis) And CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S])
			llNovoRegistro = .T.
				   	 		
		Case InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
			         CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And ;
		   	 		(!Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'S' And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S] And Not ThisForm.Editou)
			llNovoRegistro = .T.

		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And ;
					!Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And !Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons) And Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis)
			llNovoRegistro = .F.

		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And ;
				CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And ;
				CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'N' And !Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis)  And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S]
			llNovoRegistro = .T.

		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1 And CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'S'
			llNovoRegistro = .F.

		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1 And ;
				Not Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons)
			llNovoRegistro = .F.
			lnFoco = 8

		Case Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1  And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S]
			llNovoRegistro = .T.
			
	EndCase
EndIf

If llNovoRegistro
	ThisForm.NovoRegistro()
	lnFoco = 1
EndIf

Thisform.RefreshCnts()

If lnFoco # 0
	Do Case
		Case lnFoco = 1
			If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
			Else
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Bco.SetFocus
			Endif

		Case lnFoco = 2
			If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
			Else
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Age.SetFocus
			Endif

		Case lnFoco = 3
			If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
			Else
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Conta.SetFocus
			Endif
			
		Case lnFoco = 4
			If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
			Else
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Num.SetFocus
			Endif

		Case lnFoco = 5
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Valor.SetFocus


		Case lnFoco = 6
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.fweditdata_Venc.SetFocus
		
		Case lnFoco = 7
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Alinea.SetFocus
			
		Case lnFoco = 8
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel.Get_GruRes.SetFocus

	EndCase
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_conta
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 108
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero
this.Value = Transform(Val(this.Value),'@L 9999999999')

If not Empty(this.value) And not fChkDigitos(TempChq.NContas) 
	messagebox('Conta Inválida !',0+48,'')
	Return 0
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And Not ThisForm.Leitor And Empty(TempChq.Cmc7) )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Alinea
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 109
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local loBrowse

Thisform.Leitor = .F.

If !Empty(This.Value) And !Seek(This.Value,'crSigCdAli','Codigos')
	loBrowse = CreateObject( 'fwbuscasel', 'crSigCdAli', 'Codigos', This.Value, 'Códigos de Alineas' )
	loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Codigos', '999', 'Código', .t., 'Codigos' )
	loBrowse.AddColuna( 'Descrs', '', 'Descrição', .t., 'Descrs' )
	loBrowse.Show
	This.Value = Iif( LastKey() == 27, '', crSigCdAli.Codigos )
Endif	
Return (.T.)
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And CrSigCdOpt.Alineas='S' And ;
			!Empty(TempChq.Bancos))

ENDPROC

	MessageBox('Valor do Cheque Inválido',48,'')
	Return 0
EndIf

ThisForm.Achoulcheq = .f.

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.fweditdata_Venc
* Classe: fweditdata (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 111
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not IsEmpty( This.Value )
	If IsEmpty( TempChq.Datas) 
		Select TempChq
		If ( CrSigCdOpt.nDtEmiss = 2 )
			Replace TempChq.Datas With Tempchq.Vencs
		Else
			Replace TempChq.Datas with CrSigChLcq.DtEnts
		Endif
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Data_Emiss.Refresh
	Endif
	If ( Gomonth(This.Value,12) < CrSigChLcq.DtEnts Or This.Value > Gomonth(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts,12) )
		MessageBox( 'Data de Vencimento Invalida!!!', ;
					48, 'Corrija: Data de Vencimento Incorreta' )
		This.Value = {}
		This.Refresh						
		Return 0		
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.Editou = .t.
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
				(CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 Or CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1 ) And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And (Not Thisform.Achoulcheq Or ThisForm.Editou Or CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1))

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_num
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 116
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero

ThisForm.Achoulcheq = .f.

Select TempChq
lcOrder = Order()
lnRecNo = Recno()

If not Empty(this.value)
	If not fChkDigitos(TempChq.NCheques) 
		messagebox('Número Inválido !',0+48,'')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	lcBanco   = TempChq.Bancos
	lcAgencia = TempChq.Agencias
	lcConta   = TempChq.NContas
	lcNumero  = This.Value
	
	lcquery = [Select bancos,agencias,contas,ncheques,emps,cpfs,id,dopes,ncheqfin,vencs,valors,datas,cidchaves From SigCqLcq Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' ]+;
				[And Contas = ']+lcconta+[' And Ncheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
	
	=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigCqLcq')
	Select CrSigCqLcq
	If Reccount() = 0 And lcBanco = '237' &&Bradesco
		lcCt2 = '000'+Right(lcConta,7)
		lcquery = [Select bancos,agencias,contas,ncheques,emps,cpfs,id,dopes,ncheqfin,vencs,valors,datas,cidchaves From SigCqLcq Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' ]+;
					[And Contas = ']+lcCt2+[' And Ncheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrSigCqLcq')
	Endif
	If Reccount() > 0
		Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( CrSigCqLcq.Cpfs, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
		If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
			Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 999.999.999-99" )
		Else
			Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 99.999.999/9999-99" )
		EndIf
		=ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpcli','Cpfs',Cgc1,[cpfs,Iclis,Rclis,Grupos])
		Select TempChq
		Replace Vencs  With CrSigCqLcq.Vencs,;
				Valors With CrSigCqLcq.Valors,;
				GrClis With crTmpCli.Grupos

		If Empty( TempChq.Datas) 
			Select TempChq
			If ( CrSigCdOpt.nDtEmiss = 2 )
				Replace TempChq.Datas With Tempchq.Vencs
			Else
				Replace TempChq.Datas with CrSigChLcq.DtEnts
			Endif
			Thisform.CntCheque.Data_Emiss.Refresh
		Endif

		If (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1)
			Select crSigChLcq
			Replace Grclis With TempChq.Grclis, ;
					Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis

			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
				.Value = Evaluate('TempChq.Cpfs')
				.Refresh
			EndWith
		EndIf

		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crTmpCli.Iclis
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crTmpCli.Rclis
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = CrTmpCli.Cpfs

		ThisForm.Achoulcheq = .t.

	Else

		Select TempChq
		Set Order To (lcOrder)
		If lnRecNo # 0
			Goto (lnRecNo)
		Endif
			
		If Not Eof() And Not Bof()
			Skip -1
		EndIf
		
		If Not Bof() And TempChq.Bancos = lcBanco And TempChq.Agencias = lcAgencia And TempChq.nContas = lcConta

			=ThisForm.PodataMgr.Cursorquery('SigCdCli','CrTmpcli','Cpfs',TempChq.cpfs,[cpfs,Iclis,Rclis,Grupos])
	
			Select TempChq
			pVenc = Gomonth(Vencs,1)
			pVlr  = Valors
			pGrp  = GrClis
			pDt   = Datas
			pIcli = Iclis
			pCpf  = Cpfs
			Skip
			If Empty(TempChq.Vencs)
				Replace Vencs  With pVenc,;
						Valors With pVlr,;
						GrClis With pGrp
				If Empty( TempChq.Datas) 
					Replace TempChq.Datas With pDt
				Endif
			Endif
			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
				.Value = pCpf
				.Refresh
			EndWith

			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crTmpCli.Iclis
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crTmpCli.Rclis
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = CrTmpCli.Cpfs

		Else
		
			If ThisForm.tipoleitora = 'P' And CrSigCdOpt.Parcelas <> 'S'
				Select TempChq
				Set Order To (lcOrder)
				Go Bott
				Replace Iclis with '',;
						Cpfs  With ''
				
				Replace CrSigChLcq.Iclis With '' In CrSigChLcq
				
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = Space(10)
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Refresh
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.refresh
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.refresh
			Else
				Select TempChq
				Set Order To (lcOrder)
				Go Bott
				Replace Iclis with '',;
						Cpfs  With ''
				
				Replace CrSigChLcq.Iclis With '' In CrSigChLcq
				
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = Space(10)
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.Value   = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Refresh
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.refresh
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_CPF.refresh
			Endif
		
		Endif

	Endif

	=Seek(crSigChLcq.Codigos,'crSigCdOpt','Operacao')
	lnOrdem = crSigCdOpt.Ordens

	If lnOrdem = 1
		Replace marca  with 1 In TempChq	
	Else
		lcquery = [Select * From SigChe Where Bancos = ']+lcBanco+[' And Agencias = ']+lcAgencia+[' ]+;
					[And nContas = ']+lcconta+[' And Ncheques = ']+lcNumero+[']
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrCheque')

		Select TempChq
		Set Order To (lcOrder)
		If lnRecNo # 0
			Goto (lnRecNo)
		Endif

		Select CrCheque
		If Reccount() > 0
			Replace Vencs  With Crcheque.Vencs,;
					marca  with 1,;
					Valors With Crcheque.Valors In TempChq
		
		Endif
	EndIf 
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And Not ThisForm.Leitor And Empty(TempChq.Cmc7) )
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_bco
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 121
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE GotFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE GotFocus
If ThisForm.TipoLeitora = 'P'
	If ThisForm.Inicialeitura And Not ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.OutPut = 'WEU'
		=ChrSaw(.3)
		=ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.Input
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcLeitor, Contador, Tecla, lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero, lnRec, lcOrd
Local lcCheque, llCorreto, llRetBanco

llRetBanco = .T.
lcLeitor = ' '

If Not Thisform.Leitor
	If InList(ThisForm.TipoLeitora,'S','P')
		If ThisForm.IniciaLeitura
			If Empty(ThisForm.ChqLido)
				If !ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen And !ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError
					ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .T.
				EndIf
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !!!' NoWait
			Else
				If Asc(Left(ThisForm.ChqLido,1)) = 2
					lcLeitor = Substr(ThisForm.ChqLido,2,Len(ThisForm.ChqLido) - 1)
				Else
					lcLeitor = ThisForm.ChqLido
				EndIf
				ThisForm.ChqLido = ' '
			ENDIF
		ELSE
			ThisForm.ChqLido = ' '
		EndIf
	Else
		If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'K' And Lastkey() = 9
			Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !!!' nowait
			llRetorno = PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
			Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait				
			If Not Empty(crCfCom.Portas)
				llRet = PertoHabilita(crCfCom.Portas,Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				If llRet = 1
					lcLeitor = PertoAlinha(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
					If Type('lcLeitor') = 'C'
						lcLeitor = Substr(lcLeitor,5)
					Else
						lcLeitor = ''
					Endif
				EndIf
			EndIf
			If ! Empty(lcLeitor)
				llRetorno = PertoExpulsa(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
				llRetorno = PertoDesabilita(Iif(crCfCom.TpBarras=3,.t.,.f.))
			Endif
			Wait Clear
		Else			
			If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'H' And Lastkey() = 9
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !!!' nowait
				llRetorno = ChronoDesabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
				Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait				
				If Not Empty(crCfCom.Portas)
					llRet = ChronoHabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
					If llRet
						lcLeitor = ChronoAlinhaCMC7(crCfCom.Portas)
						If Type('lcLeitor') = 'C'
						Else
							lcLeitor = ''
						Endif
					EndIf
				EndIf
				If ! Empty(lcLeitor)
*						llRetorno = ChronoExpulsa(crCfCom.Portas)
					llRetorno = ChronoDesabilita(crCfCom.Portas)
				Endif
				Wait Clear
			Else			
				If !Empty(This.Value)
					lcLeitor = This.Value
					Contador = 0
					Do While LastKey() # 27 And LastKey() # 13 And LastKey() # 3 And ;
							 LastKey() # 5 And LastKey() # 18 And  LastKey() # 24 And ;
							 LastKey() # 148 And LastKey() > 0
						Tecla = LastKey()
						Contador = Contador + 1
						Read
						If Contador > 1
							lcLeitor = lcLeitor + Chr(Tecla)
						EndIf
					EndDo
				
				Else
					lcLeitor = ''
				EndIf
			EndIf
		Endif
	Endif

	If Len(Alltrim(Left(lcLeitor,34))) >= 34
		lcLeitor = Left(lcLeitor,34)
		
		ThisForm.Leitor = .T.
		If Thisform.TipoLeitora = 'K'
			lcBanco    = Substr(lcLeitor,1,3)
			This.Value = lcBanco
			This.Refresh
			lcAgencia  = Substr(lcLeitor,4,4)
			lcConta    = Substr(lcLeitor,22,10)
			lcNumero   = Substr(lcLeitor,13,6)
			lcCheque   = Substr(lcLeitor,1,3) + Substr(lcLeitor,4,4) + Substr(lcLeitor,13,6) + Substr(lcLeitor,22,10)
		Else
			lcBanco    = Substr(lcLeitor,2,3)
			This.Value = lcBanco
			This.Refresh
			lcAgencia  = Substr(lcLeitor,5,4)
			lcNumero   = Substr(lcLeitor,14,6)
			lcConta    = Substr(lcLeitor,23,10)
			lcCheque   = Substr(lcLeitor,2,3) + Substr(lcLeitor,5,4) + Substr(lcLeitor,14,6) + Substr(lcLeitor,23,10)
		Endif

		llCorreto = fVerDigitos(lcCheque,23)	

		If !llCorreto
			Thisform.Leitor = .F.
			This.Value = ''
			This.Refresh
			If InList(Thisform.TipoLeitora,'S' ,'P')
				Messagebox('Leitura Cheque Inválida !!',0+48,'')
				llRetBanco = 0
			Else
				=fMensagemFixa('Leitura Cheque Inválida !!',0+48,'',"X ")
			Endif
		Else
			If ThisForm.fVerCheque(lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero)
				Select TempChq
				Replace Bancos   with lcBanco,       ;
						Agencias with lcAgencia,     ;
						NContas  with lcConta,       ;
						NCheques with lcNumero,;
						Cmc7     With lcLeitor

				Thisform.RefreshCnts()
				=ThisForm.ValCheques()
				llRetorno = Iif(CrSigCdOpt.Alineas # 'S',0,.T.)
			Else
				Select TempChq
				This.Value = ''
				This.Refresh				
				Thisform.Limpa_Che()
				ThisForm.Leitor = .F.
				llRetBanco = 0
			EndIf
		Endif
		
		Clear TypeAHead
		
	EndIf

	If !Empty(This.Value)
		If Not fChkDigitos(TempChq.Bancos)
			This.Value = ''
			This.Refresh
			If InList(Thisform.TipoLeitora,'S' ,'P')
				Messagebox('Banco Inválido !',0+48,'')
				llRetBanco = 0
			Else		
				fMensagemFixa('Banco Inválido !',0+48,'','X ')
			Endif
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf

Select TempChq
Return llRetBanco
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
If (InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
    InList(Thisform.TipoLeitora,'S','P') And Empty(This.value) And ;
	Not ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError)
	If !ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .t.
	EndIf
	Wait Window 'Passe o cheque pela leitora !' nowait
EndIf

Thisform.Editou = .f.

Return(InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And Empty(TempChq.Cmc7))

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_ProtL
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 122
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local lcProtocolos
Declare LaCampo(2,1)

ThisForm.GetProts = .F.
If Not Empty(This.Value)
	lcProtocolos = PadL(AllTrim(This.Value),8,'0')
	If CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCqChm','CrChm','Protocolos',lcProtocolos) And ;
		   ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque','Nums',Crchm.Nums)
			Select crCheque
			Scatter Memvar
			Select TempChq
			Gather Memvar Fields Bancos, Agencias, NContas, NCheques, Leituras, Protocolos
			
			If Thisform.fVerCheque(TempChq.Bancos, TempChq.Agencias, TempChq.NContas, TempChq.NCheques)
				Thisform.NovoRegistro()
				This.Value = ''
				Return 0
			Else
				This.Value = ''
				Return 0
			EndIf
		Else
			MessageBox('Protocolo ' + lcProtocolos + ' Não Encontrado ... ',48,'')
			This.Value = ''
			Return 0
		EndIf
	EndIf
EndIf
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
This.peantvalue = This.Value

Return ( InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
         CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1 And Empty(TempChq.Bancos) )
ENDPROC

	If CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigCqChm','CrChm','Protocolos',lcProtocolos) And ;
		   ThisForm.Podatamgr.CursorQuery('SigChe','CrCheque','Nums',Crchm.Nums)

			If Thisform.fVerCheque(TempChq.Bancos, TempChq.Agencias, TempChq.NContas, TempChq.NCheques)
				Thisform.NovoRegistro()

				Return 0

				Return 0

	EndIf
EndIf

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Data_Emiss
* Classe: fweditdata (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 125
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If Not IsEmpty( This.Value )
	If ( Gomonth(This.Value,12) < CrSigChLcq.DtEnts Or This.Value > Gomonth(CrSigChLcq.DtEnts,12) )
		Messagebox( 'Período Bloqueado...', 32 )
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If Not Empty( TempChq.Vencs) And This.Value > TempChq.Vencs
		MessageBox( 'Data de Emissão do Cheque Não Pode Ser Maior Que a Data de Vencimento.', ;
					48, 'Corrija: Data de Emissão do Cheque Incorreta' )
		This.Value = {}
		This.Refresh					
		Return 0
	EndIf
Endif
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return(InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
				CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And ( Not ThisForm.Achoulcheq Or IsEmpty(This.Value) ) )

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 127
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local llFocoAuto, lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero, lcCheque, llRetorno

llRetorno = .T.
Select TempChq

llFocoAuto = .T.
lcLeitor = This.Value
*Thisform.ChqLido = ''
If CrSigCdOpt.ValidaChqs = 'S'
Else
	If InList(ThisForm.TipoLeitora,'S','P')
		If Asc(Left(lcLeitor,1)) = 2
			lcLeitor = Substr(lcLeitor,2,Len(lcLeitor) - 1)
		Else
			lcLeitor = lcLeitor
		EndIf
	Endif
	lcBanco   = Substr(lcLeitor,2,3)
	lcAgencia = Substr(lcLeitor,5,4)
	lcNumero  = Substr(lcLeitor,14,6)
	lcConta   = Substr(lcLeitor,23,10)
	lcCheque  = Substr(lcLeitor,2,3) + Substr(lcLeitor,5,4) + Substr(lcLeitor,14,6) + Substr(lcLeitor,23,10)
EndIf

If !Empty(lcBanco+lcAgencia+lcConta+lcNumero)
	If Not Thisform.fVerCheque( lcBanco, lcAgencia, lcConta, lcNumero)
		This.Value = ''
		Thisform.Limpa_Che()
		llRetorno = .F.
	Else
		Select TempChq
		Replace Bancos   With lcBanco, ;
				Agencias With lcAgencia, ;
				NContas  With lcConta, ;
				NCheques With lcNumero

		If CrSigCdOpt.Ordens # 1 And CrSigCdOpt.Alineas # 'S' And IsEmpty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons)
			Thisform.NovoRegistro()
			This.Value = ''
			llRetorno = .F.
		EndIf
	EndIf
	ThisForm.RefreshCnts()
	=ThisForm.ValCheques()
EndIf

If Not llRetorno
	If (InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
	    InList(Thisform.TipoLeitora,'S','P') And Empty(This.value) And ;
		Not ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError)
		If !ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .t.
		EndIf
		Wait Window 'Passe o Cheque pela Leitora !' NoWait
	Endif
	Return 0 
Else
	Set Confirm Off
EndIf

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Set Confirm On

If (InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
    InList(Thisform.TipoLeitora,'S','P') And Empty(This.value) And ;
    Not ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError)
	If !ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.PortOpen = .t.
	EndIf
	Wait Window 'Passe o Cheque pela Leitora !' NoWait
Endif

Return( InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
		Empty(ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_ProtL.Value) )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.btnExcluir
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 129
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Select tempchq
Go Top
Scan
	Replace Marca With 0 In Tempchq
	Select tempchq
EndScan

lnTotLote = 0
Select tempchq
lnReg = Recno()
Go Top
Scan For marca = 1
	lnTotLote = lnTotLote + tempchq.Valors
Endscan

If lnReg <> 0 And Not Eof()
	Go lnReg
Endif

This.parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Value = lnTotLote
This.parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Refresh

This.parent.cntGrade.gradeCheque.Refresh
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Inlist( ThisForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR' ) 
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.btnMarca
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 130
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Wait Window 'Aguarde, Processando cheques...' Nowait
Select tempchq
Go Top
Scan
	If Thisform.fvercheque(tempchq.Bancos, tempchq.Agencias, tempchq.nContas, tempchq.ncheques, .F., .T.)  && Rafael-31/08/2017 - Quando for marca nao para no Messagebox
		Replace DataS With Date() In tempchq
		Replace Marca With 1 In tempchq

		If crSigCdOpt.Destinos = 3
			Replace crSigChLcq.Grupos With tempchq.grclis
			Replace crSigChLcq.Contas With tempchq.iclis
			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntDestino
				If .Visible
					.get_grupo.Refresh
					.get_conta.Refresh
					.Get_dGrupo.Value = Iif(!Empty(.get_grupo.Value) And Seek(.get_grupo.Value,'crSigCdGcr','Codigos'),Evaluate('crSigCdGcr.Descrs'),'')
					.Get_dGrupo.Refresh
					lcCliente = .get_conta.Value
					lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
					=Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpcli')
					Select CrTmpCli
					Go Top
					.Get_dConta.Value = Evaluate('crtmpCli.RClis')
					.Get_dConta.Refresh
				Endif
			Endwith
			Delete From tempchq Where iclis <> crSigChLcq.Contas
		Endif
	Else
		Replace Marca With 0 In tempchq
	Endif
	Select tempchq
Endscan

Wait Clear

lnTotLote = 0
Select tempchq
lnReg = Recno()
Go Top
Scan For Marca = 1
	lnTotLote = lnTotLote + tempchq.Valors
Endscan

If lnReg <> 0 And Not Eof()
	Go lnReg
Endif

This.Parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Value = lnTotLote
This.Parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Refresh

Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntGrade.gradeCheque.Refresh
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Inlist( ThisForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR' ) 
ENDPROC

PROCEDURE Valid
Local xBrox

If !IsEmpty(This.Value)

	xBrox = CreateObject('fwbuscaext', ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn, 'SigCdGre', ;
						   'crListaRemota', 'Class', This.Value, 'Classe Empresarial', .T.)

	Else
		This.Value = crListaRemota.Class
	EndIf
	USE in crListaRemota
	This.Refresh
Else
	This.Refresh
ENDIF

thisform.MontaGrade()
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntConta
* Classe: container (Base: container)
* Registro: 139
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Do Case
	Case CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And (CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis='S' And Empty(TempChq.Cpfs) Or ;
							     Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And Empty(TempChq.Cpfs) )
		MessageBox('Conta do Emissor Inválida',48,'')
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntConta.Get_Conta.SetFocus
		
	Case !Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons)
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel.SetFocus
		
	Case Empty(crSigCdOpt.REspons) And InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
		crSigCdOpt.Obs = [S] And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos)
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntfollowUp.SetFocus

	Case InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
			!Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And Empty(CrSigCdOpt.Respons) And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S]
		Thisform.NovoRegistro()
		Thisform.RefreshCnts()
		If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
		Else
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Bco.SetFocus
		Endif

	Case Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis = 'S' And !Empty(TempChq.Cpfs) And ;
			InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha, 'INSERIR','ALTERAR')
		Thisform.NovoRegistro()
		Thisform.RefreshCnts()
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
		If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
		Else
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Bco.SetFocus
		Endif

	OtherWise
		If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
		Else
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Bco.SetFocus
		Endif
		
EndCase		

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntConta.Get_Conta
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 140
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, CrSigCdOpt.GruEmis, 'C', This.Value, This, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome, '' )
		Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
		With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta
			.Get_Conta.Value = ''
			.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
			.Get_Cpf.Value   = ''
		EndWith
	Else
		lcCliente = This.Value
		lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos,situas,VultComps,UltComps From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
		=Thisform.PodataMgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
		Select CrTmpCli
		Go Top
		With ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta
			Select TempChq
			Replace Grclis With crtmpCli.Grupos
			This.Value      = crtmpCli.Iclis
			.Get_Cpf.Value  = crtmpCli.Cpfs
			.Get_Nome.Value = crtmpCli.Rclis
			This.Refresh
			.Get_Cpf.Refresh
			.Get_Nome.Refresh
			
			If not fChecarInativas(This.Value,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
				This.Value = ''
				.Get_Cpf.Value  = ''
				.Get_Nome.Value = ''
				Return(.F.)
			Endif 											
		EndWith
		
		If (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1)
			Select crSigChLcq
			Replace Grclis With TempChq.Grclis, ;
					Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis

			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
				.Value = Evaluate('TempChq.Cpfs')
				.Refresh
			EndWith
		EndIf
		
		lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCst Where Codigos=']+CrtmpCli.situas+[']
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCst')
		If CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis = 'S' And crSigCdCst.DispMovs = 1
			_Msg = 'Situação      : ' + crtmpCli.Situas + ' - ' + crSigCdCst.Descrs + chr(13) + ;
	               'Última Compra : ' + Alltrim(Str(crtmpCli.VultComps,12,2)) + ' / ' + Dtoc(Nvl(crtmpCli.UltComps,{})) 
			=MessageBox( _msg, 48, '' )
		Endif

	EndIf	
Else
	If !Empty(This.peantvalue)
		Select TempChq
		Replace GrClis With '', ;
				Iclis  With '', ;
				Cpfs   With '', ;
				Nomes  With ''
	EndIf
Endif

ThisForm.RefreshCnts()

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
This.peantvalue = This.Value

llOk = ( InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
         (CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 or CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1) And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) And ;
	   	 ( (Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis) And CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S') Or ;
	   	    (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'S' And (Not Thisform.achoulcheq Or ThisForm.Editou)) Or ( CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis = 'S' And Empty(TempChq.Cpfs) ) ) )
Return ( llOk )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntConta.Get_CPF
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 141
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
Local ArqSel,Cgc
If !Empty(This.Value)
	Cgc = This.Value
	Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( Cgc, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
	If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
		Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 999.999.999-99" )
	Else
		Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 99.999.999/9999-99" )
	EndIf

	lcQuery = [Select Iclis, Cpfs, Rclis, Grupos, Situas, vultcomps, ultcomps From SigCdCli Where Cpfs = ?Cgc1 ]
	lnErro = ThisForm.podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'CrtmpCli')
	
	If lnErro < 1
		MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis <> [S]

		Select CrtmpCli
		Go Top
		If Reccount() = 0
			m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',Thisform.poDatamgr.pnIdconn,'SigCdCli','crtmpcli','cpfs',This.Value,'Clientes',1000)
			If .Not.m.loLista.plAchouRegistro
				m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cpfs' ,'', 'C.P.F. / C.N.P.J.' )
				m.loLista.mAddColuna('Rclis',Replicate('x',50), 'Nome' )
				m.loLista.mAddColuna('Iclis','', 'Código' )
				m.loLista.mAddColuna('Inativas ', .f., .f., .f., .f., .f., .t.)
				m.loLista.gridProc.SetAll('DynamicForeColor', [Iif(crAcSigCdCli.Inativas == 1, Rgb(255, 0, 0), Rgb(0,0,0))], 'Column')
				m.loLista.Show()
			EndIf
		EndIf
		This.Value = crtmpCli.Cpfs
		Cgc = This.Value
		Cgc = Strt( Strt( Strt( Cgc, ".", "" ), "-", "" ), "/", "" )
		If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
			Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 999.999.999-99" )
		Else
			Cgc1 = TransForm( Cgc, "@R 99.999.999/9999-99" )
		EndIf
		Select TempChq
		Replace GrClis With crtmpCli.Grupos
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crtmpCli.Iclis
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crtmpCli.Rclis
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Refresh
		If not fChecarInativas(crTmpCli.iClis,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
			MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = ''
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value   = '' 
			Return(.F.)
		Endif 				
	Else
		Select CrtmpCli
		Go Top
		If Reccount() > 0
			This.Value = crtmpCli.Cpfs
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crtmpCli.Iclis
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = crtmpCli.Rclis
			
			If not fChecarInativas(crTmpCli.iClis,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
				MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
				ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value   = '' 
				Return(.F.)
			Endif 							
			
			This.Refresh
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Refresh
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Refresh
			Select TempChq
			Replace GrClis With crtmpCli.Grupos

			If (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1)
				Select crSigChLcq
				Replace Grclis With TempChq.Grclis, ;
						Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis

				With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
					.Value = Evaluate('TempChq.Cpfs')
					.Refresh
				EndWith
			EndIf

			* Mostra dados da ultima compra do funcionario

			lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCst Where Codigos=']+CrtmpCli.situas+[']
			ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCst')
			If CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis = 'S' And crSigCdCst.DispMovs = 1
				_Msg = 'Situação      : ' + crtmpCli.Situas + ' - ' + crSigCdCst.Descrs + chr(13) + ;
		               'Última Compra : ' + Alltrim(Str(crtmpCli.VultComps,12,2)) + ' / ' + Dtoc(Nvl(crtmpCli.UltComps,{})) 
				=MessageBox( _msg, 48, '' )
			Endif

		Else
			Select TempChq
			Replace GrClis With ''
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value = Cgc1
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Refresh

			_Msg = 'Situação : CLIENTE NÃO CADASTRADO !!!!'
			=MessageBox( _msg, 48, '' )

		EndIf


	EndIf

	If Len(Alltrim(Cgc))<>14
		If !fValidarCPF( Alltrim( Cgc1 ) )
			Messagebox('CPF Inválido ! ',0+64,"Atenção !!!")
			Return(.f.)
		EndIf
	Else
		If !fValidarCNPJ( Alltrim( Cgc1 ) )
			Messagebox('CPF Inválido ! ',0+64,"Atenção !!!")
			Return(.f.)
		EndIf
	EndIf

EndIf

Thisform.RefreshCnts()

Return .T.

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return ( InList(Thisform.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And ;
		 (CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 Or CrSigCdOpt.AltDados = 1) And Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And ;
	   ( (Empty(crSigChLcq.Iclis) And CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S') Or CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds = 'S' ) And ;
  		 !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) ) 
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntConta.Get_Nome
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 142
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value) And Empty(ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value) And ;
			Empty(ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, CrSigCdOpt.GruEmis, 'D', This.Value, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta, This )
		Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = ''
		Return 0
	Else	
		lcCliente = ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.Cntconta.Get_conta.value
		lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos,situas,VultComps,UltComps From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
		=ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcsql,'CrTmpcli')
		Select CrTmpCli
		Go Top
		This.Refresh
		
		If not fChecarInativas(crTmpCli.iClis,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
			MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = ''
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Nome.Value  = ''
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value   = '' 
			Return(.F.)
		Endif 				
		
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Value   =  crtmpCli.Cpfs
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Cpf.Refresh
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Value = crtmpCli.Iclis
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntConta.Get_Conta.Refresh
		Select TempChq
		Replace GrClis With crtmpCli.Grupos

		If (CrSigCdOpt.EmiInds # 'S' And CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1)
			Select crSigChLcq
			Replace Grclis With TempChq.Grclis, ;
					Iclis  With TempChq.Iclis

			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntEmissor.Get_Cpf
				.Value = Evaluate('TempChq.Cpfs')
				.Refresh
			EndWith
		EndIf

		* Mostra dados da ultima compra do funcionario

		lcWher = [Select * From SigCdCst Where Codigos=']+CrtmpCli.situas+[']
		ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'CrSigCdCst')
		If CrSigCdOpt.CadEmis = 'S' And crSigCdCst.DispMovs = 1
			_Msg = 'Situação      : ' + crtmpCli.Situas + ' - ' + crSigCdCst.Descrs + chr(13) + ;
	               'Última Compra : ' + Alltrim(Str(crtmpCli.VultComps,12,2)) + ' / ' + Dtoc(Nvl(crtmpCli.UltComps,{})) 
			=MessageBox( _msg, 48, '' )
		Endif

	Endif
Endif

ENDPROC

		 CrSigCdOpt.Ordens = 1 And Empty(TempChq.Iclis) And ;

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel
* Classe: container (Base: container)
* Registro: 144
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
Do Case
	Case Empty(TempChq.GruRes)
		MessageBox('Grupo do Responsável Inválido',48,'')
		This.Get_GruRes.SetFocus		
		
	Case Empty(TempChq.ConRes)
		MessageBox('Nome do Responsável é Obrigatório',48,'')
		This.Get_ConRes.SetFocus		
	
	Case !Empty(TempChq.GruRes) And !Empty(TempChq.ConRes) And Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And crSigCdOpt.Obs # [S]
		ThisForm.NovoRegistro()
		Thisform.RefreshCnts()
		ThisForm.Manual=.f.
		If CrSigCdOpt.TpLeituras = 2
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Cheque.SetFocus
		Else
			ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_Bco.SetFocus
		EndIf

	Case !Empty(TempChq.GruRes) And !Empty(TempChq.ConRes) And Thisform.pcEscolha = 'INSERIR' And crSigCdOpt.Obs = [S]
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntfollowUp.Get_obs.Setfocus
	
EndCase

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel.Get_ConRes
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 146
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_GruRes.Value, 'C', This.Value, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes )
		Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value = ''
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Value = ''
	EndIf
	If not fChecarInativas(This.Value,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
		MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value = ''
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Value = '' 
		Return(.F.)
	Endif 				
Else
	ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Value = ''
EndIf

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Refresh
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Refresh

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return ( InList(ThisForm.pcEscolha,'INSERIR','ALTERAR') And !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) )
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CntResponsavel.Get_dConRes
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 149
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty(This.Value)
	If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, This.Value, 'D', This.Value, ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes, This )
		Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value = ''
		This.Value = ''
	EndIf
	If not fChecarInativas(ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value,ThisForm.Podatamgr)
		MessageBox('Conta Inativa ...', 32, '')
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value = ''
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Value = '' 
		Return(.F.)
	Endif 				
Else
	ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value  = ''
EndIf

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Refresh
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_dConRes.Refresh
ENDPROC

* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------

		 Empty(ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.cntResponsavel.Get_ConRes.Value) And ;
		 !Empty(TempChq.Bancos) )

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.CmdExcCheque
* Classe: fwbtng (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 160
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lcCheque, lnRecNo

lnRecNo = 0

Select TempChq
If !Eof()
	Delete
	If !Eof()
		lnRecNo = RecNo('TempChq')
	Else
		Skip - 1
		If !Bof()
			lnRecNo = RecNo('TempChq')
		EndIf
	Endif
	ThisForm.NovoRegistro()
	If lnRecNo # 0
		Select TempChq
		Goto (lnRecNo)
	Else
		Goto Bottom
	EndIf
		
	=ThisForm.ValCheques()
	ThisForm.RefreshCnts()
Else
	ThisForm.NovoRegistro()
EndIf

ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntGrade.GradeCheque.Banco.Setfocus
ENDPROC


================================================================================

		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.OutPut = 'WE'
	EndIf
Else
	ThisForm.Inicialeitura = .F.
	If InList(ThisForm.TipoLeitora,'P','S') And Not ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.PortaError
		ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.ReadComm.Ole_Comm.OutPut = 'WE'
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.Pagina.Dados.CntCheque.Get_bco.SetFocus

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGCDLCH.Pagina.Dados.cntGrade.GradeCheque.Marca.Check1
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 192
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
RETURN Thisform.pcEscolha='INSERIR'	&& permite só na inclusão
ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
*** mena - trata seleção de cheques ***
If This.Value = 1 
	If Thisform.fvercheque(tempchq.Bancos, tempchq.Agencias, tempchq.nContas, tempchq.ncheques)
		Replace DataS With Date() In tempchq
		If crSigCdOpt.Destinos = 3
			Replace crSigChLcq.Grupos With tempchq.grclis
			Replace crSigChLcq.Contas With tempchq.iclis
			With Thisform.Pagina.Dados.CntDestino
				If .Visible
					.get_grupo.Refresh
					.get_conta.Refresh
					.Get_dGrupo.Value = Iif(!Empty(.get_grupo.Value) And Seek(.get_grupo.Value,'crSigCdGcr','Codigos'),Evaluate('crSigCdGcr.Descrs'),'')
					.Get_dGrupo.Refresh
					lcCliente = .get_conta.Value
					lcSql = [Select Iclis,Rclis,Cpfs,grupos From SigCdCli where Iclis = ']+lcCliente+[']
					=Thisform.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'CrTmpcli')
					Select CrTmpCli
					Go Top
					.Get_dConta.Value = Evaluate('crtmpCli.RClis')
					.Get_dConta.Refresh
				Endif
			Endwith
			Delete From tempchq Where iclis <> crSigChLcq.Contas
			Thisform.Pagina.Dados.cntGrade.gradeCheque.Refresh
		Endif
	Else
		This.Value = 0
	Endif
Endif

lnTotLote = 0
Select tempchq
lnReg = Recno()
Go Top
Scan For marca = 1
	lnTotLote = lnTotLote + tempchq.Valors
Endscan

If lnReg <> 0 
	Go lnReg
Endif

This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Value = lnTotLote
This.Parent.Parent.Parent.Parent.CntInfos.Get_Valor.Refresh

ENDPROC
